(c) The nightly soak: `nightly.yml` gained a `soak` job, `tools/scripts/soak.py --only user` (the five user rows, 60 minutes of programme each, about 12× real time here, so about 5 minutes per row) with its summary on the run page and the JSON kept; the fuzz row stays out (the bypass reference's limiter, above). The rows still find clicks: 2 minutes each here, on the working tree of this batch, read 0 / 3 / 0 / 2 / 5 clicks (Music, Gaming, Competitive FPS, Late Night, 44.1 kHz; 5 of them while bypassed; e.g. −43 dBFS 151 ms after a bank switch at Boost 99 %, −47.6 dBFS 121 ms after `comp.ratio = 9.7:1`), not triaged, so the soak step fails without failing the nightly run until they are. Open: that triage (the Done-when's 0 discontinuities), pluginval at 10 on Windows and macOS (first CI run), `readWav` and the JUCE-side parsers in the fuzzers, steps 0 and 5 and the real-time soak.
- **Status (local, 2026-10-01: the soak's click triage).** Done for the five user rows (2 minutes each, Windows / MSVC 19.51: the same 12 clicks at the same frames and levels as gcc on Linux). Method: each click's frame is re-rendered through a fresh chain held static at the parameters in force there, from 4 s before (a click that comes back is the processing's own response to the programme; one that does not comes from a change), then by module (one off, or only one on) and, for the transition, by withholding parts of the preset switch from an otherwise identical run. **Fixed (2 clicks):** `user-gaming` 92.64 s, 9 ms after a preset switch to *Voice Chat*: needed EQ band 1's type change (64 Hz bell → 110 Hz low cut), the compressor switching on (+16 dB make-up) and the continuous moves together; the EQ's discrete-change crossfade was a linear ramp whose start and end are breaks in the slope of the band's contribution. It is now a smoothstep (`ParametricEq.cpp`): on loud bass the largest 4th difference during a switch is 72.8 dB over steady state with the linear ramp, 28.8 dB with the smoothstep (*ParametricEq: a type switch crossfades without corners …*); static renders, golden files and the render baseline do not move (the fade only runs on a change). **Left (10), none a transition glitch:** (a) 4 clicks, `user-night` 107.34 s and `user-44k` 20.02 s: the static render clicks on the same frame and Punch off removes it - E04's high-band onset lift (`clarity.attackHigh`) on a kick, the processing's own sound; a slower high-band smoothing would move every Punch render (an owner decision); (b) 1 click, `user-gaming` 97.05 s: the static render clicks with only the EQ on (a 35 Hz low cut, +1 dB at band 4, a 10 kHz high cut), a linear filter, so it is the programme's own onset read 26 dB over a residual the high cut lowered (threshold 24): a detector false positive; (c) 5 clicks while the global bypass is engaged (`user-night` 90.71 s, `user-44k` 65.21 / 65.63 s): the automation had set `input.gain` to +21 – 22 dB, so the bypass reference (post input stage) runs through its ceiling limiter at 6 – 12 dB of gain reduction (its safety clamp never engaged); the static bypassed renders click the same way at other moments. User rows: 12 → 10 clicks. The `fuzz` row (2 minutes, every parameter random) reads 6 clicks before and after the change; its triage is the next line (the actions named before them are not their cause).
- **Status (local, fuzz row triage).** The fuzz row's 6 clicks (seed 6, 2 minutes, reproduced bit for bit on MSVC) are **0 transition glitches**: 5 are class (a), the processing's own sound, and 1 is class (c). The last actions the report names (eq.1.q, eq.1.gain, eq.8.type, max.ceiling) are coincidental: the EQ module was off for clicks 1–4, the bypass was engaged for click 5. Evidence (a scratch harness re-running the soak's automation against `flub_core`): with every action after 16.0 s skipped, the 16.12 / 16.33 s clicks remain, and a static render at those settings clicks 8 times in 4.3 s on the Speech scene's syllable onsets; with Punch's onset lift off (macro.1, clarity.attack, clarity.attackHigh at 0) they are gone. Click 6 (91.53 s) is a kick in the Music scene at Punch 77 % and Boost 92 %: its 4th-difference spike is there whether the ceiling moves or not (5.5e-3 / 4.2e-3; the move only tips the verdict). Click 5 (80.10 s) is the bypass reference's limiter with input.gain +9.4 dB and a −9.75 dB ceiling (class (c)). Smoother shapes are not the cause: a smoothstep ceiling glide leaves all 6, and slower band-shaper gain smoothing only moves them. It is reachable with the plain macros: Boost 66 % + Punch 53 % on the defaults clicks 8 times in the 6 s Speech scene (largest 38.8 dB over the 24 dB threshold), 0 with either at 0 (*KnownGap: Punch 53 % with Boost 66 % reads as clicks on the soak's speech voice ...*, `tests/test_soak.cpp`). So 5 of the fuzz row's and 4 of the user rows' clicks are one owner decision: accept Punch's onset lift into a Boost-driven maximizer as its sound, or re-voice it (18 factory presets set macro.1). No DSP, render or golden change.
- **Status (owner-verified, 2026-10-06, Turtle Beach Stealth 600PC Gen 3, USB dongle, Windows 11).** The Punch clicks are audible on real hardware: on a spoken podcast through per-app capture (Edge on the Music strip) at Boost about 70 % and Punch 60 – 100 %, the owner hears the ticks on syllable onsets that the soak triage found.
- **Status (local, Windows, owner decision 2026-10-06: Punch at high Boost).** Decided and done ([E04](#e04) Status, owner decision 2026-10-06): Punch's attack rows fade out from Boost 60 to 70 % (`MacroMap::punchHighBoostScale`), bit-identical below; speech ticks at medium Boost (45 – 60 %) are accepted by the owner. Fresh-chain scenes: Music Boost 100 + Punch 100 4 → 0 clicks, Boost 72 + Punch 77 2 → 0; Loud Boost 72 + Punch 77 9 → 0; Speech Boost 66 + Punch 53 8 → 4 (the KnownGap case stays, accepted). The soak (`soak.py --minutes 2`, MSVC, the same build before → after): user rows 0 / 1 / 0 / 4 / 5 → 0 / 3 / 0 / 4 / 5 (10 → 12), fuzz row 6 → 9. Gone: the fuzz row's Music kick at Punch 77 / Boost 92 (91.53 s, 1) and its Speech syllables at 16.33 s (2; Boost 66 + Punch 53, the rows now at 35 %). New: `user-gaming` 24.01 s (2), Music at Boost 85.6 % with Punch 0 (its bank) as the Loud scene ends; the fuzz row's 72.26 / 72.69 s (4), Boost 92.5 % with Punch 36 %, whose rows are now 0; and its 18.01 s (2), the Loud scene's start at Boost 66 % + Punch 53 % (rows at 35 %). Six of the eight new ones come with Punch adding nothing: the soak carries its history (governor, levels, the maximizer's state), so taking Punch's lift out at high Boost moves where the maximizer's own clicks land rather than removing them. The two class (a) clicks above remain: `user-night` 107.34 s at the same level (−40.37 dB), and `user-44k` 20.02 s (bank A at Boost 98.9 %) with Punch's attack rows at 0 there (−48.22 → −46.34 dB), so that one is not Punch's either. Boost alone on a fresh chain reads clicks in the Loud scene from 75 % (2 / 5 / 1 / 2 / 3 at 75 / 80 / 85 / 92 / 100) and 1 – 2 in the Music scene at 92 – 92.5 %, none in the Speech and Game scenes: the maximizer's gain at full drive (gone with Boost's `max.drive` row off; the clipper is not it), a new triage item (*KnownGap: Music Punch at high Boost - the soak's Loud scene …*). Gaming, checked and not changed: Footsteps / Detail 0 – 100 % at Boost 0 – 100 read 0 clicks in the Game scene, and in the Loud scene 0 up to Boost 80 %; at Boost 92 / 100 the Loud scene reads 0 – 2 (Boost alone 1 / 0; Footsteps 30 – 100 % 0 / 2 / 0 / 1 and 0 / 2 / 0 / 0; Detail 2 / 1 / 0 / 0 and 0 / 2 / 2 / 2), the maximizer at full drive as in Music; left for the owner (Detail at Boost 100 is the one that adds clicks).
- **Status (local, Windows, 2026-10-07, the real-device soak).** Done: step 4 on a real device (R1.5). **Tool.** `Flubsound Pro --device-soak --device "<output>"` (`app/Source/shell/DeviceSoak.*`, [06 §11.1](06-gui.md#111-headless-real-device-soak-11-e53-r15); `tools/scripts/device-soak.py` runs the rows) plays the full app engine (`EngineController` → `AudioEngineHost` → `MixEngine`) on one output for N minutes with temporary settings: the output is pinned (`AudioEngineHost::setOutputPin`: the host only ever asks for that output, never the system default; a device JUCE opens by itself under another name - its own fallback - plays silence from its first callback, is closed and ends the run), the Game strip gets the `TestSignalGenerator`'s 7.1 game scene and the Music strip its music (−6 dB each) inside the device callback (`setDeviceSignalSource`), and one host action every 2 s ± 50 % goes through the same `EngineController` calls as the UI and the hotkeys (15 kinds from a seeded bag: factory preset, Boost, macro, master and strip bypass, A/B, mute, strip gain, latency profile = a crossfaded engine swap, mode, a module's ear, Night, Focus, protection strength, Smart macros). The engine's output as the callback hands it to the device (what the device then does with it, e.g. an underrun, is not seen) is copied in the callback into a preallocated `flub::StreamTap` (`core/include/flub/analysis/StreamTap.h`, `FLUB_NONBLOCKING`, listed in `tests/test_rtsan.cpp`) and read at 50 Hz on the message thread by a `DiscontinuityDetector` (a second one reads the dry programme); the report adds the callback timing (`CallbackTiming`), the device's and JUCE's xrun counts, JUCE's, the process's and the system's CPU, device restarts and errors, overload episodes, engine swaps, the fold's headroom limiter, and private bytes every 10 s. Each detection is classed (restart, gap, headroom, transition ≤ 500 ms after an action, programme, static). `--replay <report.json>` re-runs a report's session on a virtual device (the same rate, buffer, programme and every action at its logged frame, 3.5× faster than real time here) and lists which detections came back; `--dump t` writes the output around a time as a WAV plus both strips' states. Tests: `tests/test_stream_tap.cpp` (4 cases), `tests/app/test_app_device_soak.cpp` (6 cases: the command line, the source and tap in the callback with 0 allocations / frees / locks, the pin, a 2.4 s virtual soak with every action kind and the report's fields, an injected one-sample impulse found and classed static, a replay that reproduces every action and detection). **Measured** on the owner's PC (Windows 11, MSVC 19.51 Release), output "Digital Audio (S/PDIF) (High Definition Audio Device)" (nothing connected, silent; the Stealth headset and the HDMI outputs were never opened), 48 kHz, programme and actions as above; the machine was busy with other builds throughout (system CPU 49–100 %, per row below), and this session's own builds and replays ran beside rows 1–4 (builds also during row 4): (1) Low Latency Mode, its default 480 samples (10 ms), 10 min: 60 002 callbacks, duration mean 2.48 / p99.9 4.61 / max 6.59 ms (66 % of the period), interval max 12.07 ms, **0 late, 0 over budget**, JUCE glitches 0, JUCE load mean 25 % (max 40 %), process 33 % of one core, system 99 %; 304 actions (every kind ≥ 15), 16 engine swaps; 0 clicks, 0 dropouts, 0 NaN, 0 tap gaps, 0 restarts / errors; 6 DC-step readings (3 moments × 2 channels). (2) Low Latency Mode at the smallest buffer the device offers, 128 samples (2.67 ms), 10 min: 225 005 callbacks, mean 0.80 / p99.9 3.06 / max 4.19 ms (157 %), **896 late (0.40 %), 837 over budget (0.37 %)**, JUCE glitches 850, 5 overload episodes, system 94 %; 297 actions, 14 swaps; 1 click, 4 DC-step readings, 0 dropouts / NaN / gaps / restarts. 151 of the 176 seconds with an overrun lie in 143–323 s, while this session linked a build and then ran a 10-minute replay beside the soak, 18 in 12–39 s (not identified; a build of this session may still have been compiling), 7 elsewhere; in the 277 s after the replay ended, 2 seconds had an overrun and none a late callback; a rerun with the same seed while this session ran nothing beside it (system still 78 % busy with other builds): mean 0.71 / p99.9 1.91 ms (72 % of the period), **16 late and 19 over budget (0.008 %)**, 13 of them in 164–195 s and one stall of 11.6 ms (4.3 periods) at 44 s, 2 overload episodes, and the same 5 detections at the same frames and levels. So 128 samples keeps about 28 % headroom at p99.9 on this machine but not against bursts of competing load, while 480 samples had 0 overruns in 50 minutes. (3) Shared mode ("Windows Audio", 480 samples fixed), Balanced, 10 min: 60 003 callbacks, max 6.97 ms (70 %), **0 late, 0 over budget, 0 detections** (clean), system 76 %. (4) The first-run configuration (Low Latency Mode, 480, Balanced), **30 min**: 180 003 callbacks, mean 1.74 / p99.9 4.56 / max 7.37 ms (74 %), interval max 12.51 ms, **0 late, 0 over budget, 0 detections of any kind**, 0 restarts / errors, 893 actions (every kind ≥ 44), 45 engine swaps, JUCE load mean 17 % (max 40 %), system 49 %. Memory: private bytes 18.4–24.4 MB in every row, a saw-tooth that follows the latency profile the automation picked (each switch builds a new engine); the lowest value of the last third against the first third after the first minute +1.18 / +1.62 / −1.38 / −0.97 MB (rows 1–4), so no growth in 30 min. JUCE's WASAPI types count xruns only on an input (−1 here: output only), so device underruns are not counted; a late callback is a candidate underrun, not a proven one. **Triage** (the E53 method: re-render, then take parts away; here with `--replay` on the virtual device): every detection of rows 1 and 2 came back in the replay (6 / 6 and 5 / 5 by the replay's comparison: same type within one block + 3 ms of the same programme frame; checked afterwards from the reports: the same frame exactly and within 0.01 dB), so **none came from the real-time path**. (a) The 10 DC-step readings are all at −30.0 dB, the detector's threshold, 77–367 ms after an explosion of the game scene (its 36 Hz LFE burst and 260 Hz body move the 2 Hz low-pass by about the threshold; the dry programme alone crosses it twice in 10 min, and in the 128-sample rerun the fold headroom of (b) acted at each of them): the explosion's low end read at the detector's limit, not a glitch (the replay of row 1 reads two more at another explosion, after a profile switch that the replay starts up to a block earlier than the device did). (b) The click (row 2, 199.680 s, −26.45 dBFS, +24.1 dB over the 24 dB threshold, Game strip): the virtualiser's fold headroom ([E28](#e28)a: zero latency, instant attack at 0 dBFS, 10 ms hold, 150 ms release) re-acting during an explosion that drove the binaural fold 1–5.4 dB over 0 dBFS for 0.7 s (`MeterBus::foldHeadroomDb`, read after every block in the replay): the click comes in the block where the gain stepped from −3.63 to −3.79 dB. It needs the Game strip's bank B (A/B switch 8.2 s before; that bank holds AutoLevel at −14 LUFS), stays without the mode switch 0.4 s before, without the master and the strip bypass and with every other module's ear held, scales with the strip gain (−12 dB instead of +3 dB: −44.6 dBFS at the same +24.4 dB), and goes with the virtualiser's ear held or AutoLevel off. So it is the processing's own response to a full-scale correlated 7.1 explosion lifted by AutoLevel, not a transition or real-time glitch; a soft attack or a short look-ahead for the fold headroom would move only renders of such overs (owner decision; not changed). No real-time-path bug was found, so no DSP or engine code changed (no render, golden file or render baseline moves). Open: captures, device inputs and the drift FIFO on a real device (the soak feeds the strips itself), a 128-sample run on an idle machine, the Linux and macOS devices, the weekly run.
- **Status (local, Windows, 2026-10-07, review fixes of the real-device soak).** A review of the tool found crashes, a test that could not fail and claims beyond the evidence; fixed, with before → after: (1) `--dump t` less than 0.5 s into the stream indexed the history ring with a negative frame: `--device-soak --replay <report> --dump 0.1` crashed (the reviewer's segfault; here the new test's process dies when the old start of the copy is put back) → the file now starts at the stream's first frame (a 0.6 s file for 0.1 s; on the S/PDIF output a 30 s smoke with `--dump 0.3,0.05` wrote 0.8 s and 0.55 s files and exited 0), and `--dump` refuses negative or non-numeric times (exit 2; `abc` was read as 0); the report lists every dump (`dumps`: file, span, or not written). (2) `--replay` of a report whose action names a strip or parameter that does not exist (a macro with param −100000) crashed → refused with exit 2 and the action named; `describe()` no longer indexes the parameter layout out of range and `apply()` skips strips the layout lacks. (3) A stalled callback at the end could outlive the programme and the tap (the host's bounded 250 ms wait was ignored) → `setDeviceSignalSource` / `setOutputTap` return whether the callback passed, and the soak now closes its device first (removing the host's callback waits on JUCE's callback lock for the one in flight) and only then detaches and frees them; a test stalls a callback on a second thread and shows the clear reporting false and `closeDevice()` returning only after the callback left. The virtual clock no longer waits out that 250 ms at every end: the short virtual run 0.79 → 0.43 s, the replay test 1.06 → 0.44 s, the pulse test 0.61 → 0.29 s. (4) A stopped virtual device spun the replay at 100 % CPU for ever → the soak ends `aborted` (exit 4) when the virtual device runs no callback or 64 steps bring no frame (test: < 0.05 s). (5) The replay test's original run had 0 detections, so its detection checks held as 0 == 0 → it now injects a pulse at 0.2 s (the report carries `injectPulseAtSeconds`, `--replay` repeats it): 2 detections in the original, both back at the same frame and level; a new case puts a pulse 100 ms after a scripted protection change and checks the `transition` class and the named action (no test covers `headroom`, `programme`, `gap` or `restart` yet). (6) `for (const juce::StringArray bad : …)` in the test copies per row, which gcc 11+ / clang reject under `-Wall -Werror` (`-Wrange-loop-construct`; MSVC has no such warning) → `const auto&`; the bundled clang-tidy 22 (clang front end, `-Wall -Wextra -Wshadow -Wrange-loop-construct`) reported exactly that warning before and nothing on the changed app files after; the core files are clean under the core flags (`-Wpedantic -Wconversion`). gcc and Apple Clang have still not compiled this code (not pushed). (7) Safety: the soak refuses the system default output unless `--allow-audible` is given (exit 3, nothing opened; `--device-soak --list --device "Speakers (Stealth 600PC Gen 3)"` reports that refusal on the owner's PC without opening a stream), and prints that it plays up to full scale; the temporary settings folder is no longer created (nothing is written there), so a killed soak leaves nothing in %TEMP% (0 folders before and after the runs here); on Windows the soak attaches to the console it was started from when it has no standard output at all. A probe from a fresh console (no redirection) showed the `--list` text arriving both with and without that attach: launched directly or through `powershell -Command` it printed into that console, through `cmd /c` into the handles cmd itself had been given - a windowed child inherits its parent's standard handles here - so the reported "invisible in cmd or PowerShell" was not reproduced, and the attach (for a start with no handle at all) is untested; an interactive prompt does not wait for the windowed program, so run it through `tools/scripts/device-soak.py` or with a redirection. (8) Wording: the tap is the engine's output handed to the device, so "0 dropouts" says nothing about device underruns (late callbacks are the proxy); the pin keeps audio off every other output but cannot stop JUCE's own fallback from opening one for a moment (silenced, then closed); the rows ran on earlier builds of `DeviceSoak.cpp` / `FlubsoundApplication.cpp` (the real-time path, `AudioEngineHost` and `StreamTap`, unchanged since), whose classifier said `transition 5` for row 2 where the final one (the quiet rerun) says `headroom 5`. No real-time path, DSP, golden file or render baseline changed. Tests: 6 new app cases (12 E53 app cases in all, each ≤ 0.44 s); full `ctest` on the final build: `flub_tests` passed (363.8 s) and `flub_app_tests` passed (37.1 s; 322 / 322 cases after a comment-only rebuild); MSVC 19.51 with warnings as errors builds with no warnings.
- **Status (local, Windows, 2026-10-07, the soak merged with the latency work R1.1).** Merged with *Automatic buffer size* ([E42](#e42) E42c, on by default), the soak no longer kept its buffer: its temporary settings store no choice, so the upgrade path turned Automatic on whenever the device opened at its default size; the host then re-opened the soak's device at the latency profile's size (on the virtual device 480 → 256 samples at Balanced, `--buffer` ignored), and every Profile action and glitch back-off step re-opened it again, each a device restart and so a finding (exit 1). Before → after: the short virtual run and its `--replay` failed (`bufferSize` 256 instead of 480, restarts, fewer engine swaps) → pass (480, 0 restarts, every profile switch an engine swap). Fix: `DeviceSoak::makeEngineOptions` turns Automatic off in `beforeDeviceOpen` on both clocks, before the device opens, so `--buffer` (or the device default) holds for the whole run. New test: `App: E53 device soak: both clocks keep the buffer asked for (Automatic buffer size off); a profile switch swaps the engine, never restarts the device` (the real-device clock with the virtual type standing in for a managed device type; without the fix it fails: Automatic on, 256 samples, a device start per switch). Also from the merge review: the output tap now copies the output after a latency probe (as handed to the device), a probe stays silent on a device that is not the pinned output, and *Measure latency…* is refused while an output pin or a device signal source is set (a soak); the output-pin test no longer reads past the virtual device's block. Not yet re-run on a real device.
- **What.** (0) A manual first-execution campaign on real Windows 10 22H2 and Windows 11 PCs. (1) libFuzzer + ASan targets for `json::parse`, `readWav`, `preset::fromJson`, `DeviceProfiles` and `stripStateFromJson`. (2) A randomised full-chain automation fuzz across block sizes and rates. (3) pluginval at strictness 10 plus plug-in state round-trip tests on three OSes. (4) A soak harness with a discontinuity detector (1 h nightly, 8 h per release). (5) A self-hosted Windows runner job for process loopback, MMCSS, autostart and the version-guarded `IPolicyConfig` vtable; a macOS smoke test.
- **Why.** Every stability claim rests on short synthetic runs. About 2800 lines of Windows and macOS code have never executed, including [PlatformServices_win.cpp][PSW] (2147 lines: session enumeration, process-loopback capture, default-endpoint switching through an assumed `IPolicyConfig` slot 25, whose own comment warns "A wrong vtable layout would crash the calling process"). The plug-in has zero tests. The CI app job runs against a fake device and router only.
- **Expected benefit.** The gating item for any gaming claim: [E50](#e50) Phase B, [E51](#e51), [E55](#e55) and [E56](#e56) cannot be validated without it. A 10-minute offline CLI soak was stable, so the risk sits in the real-time and device layers.
- **Approach.** Step 0 first, with a written checklist in docs/10 and results recorded in TRACEABILITY R4.4/R4.5; budget 1–3 weeks for fixing what first execution breaks. Fuzz 5 min per target per PR and 1 h nightly, seeded from presets and test WAVs, replacing the fixed-seed loops in `test_json.cpp` and `test_wav.cpp`. Automation fuzz asserts finite output, true peak ≤ ceiling + 0.1 dB and no RTSan violation. Soak: a headless `--soak` mode on a PipeWire null sink with a 997 Hz phase-locked predictor (residual above −60 dBFS counts as a glitch), plus the same soak on the Windows runner. Land pluginval as non-blocking for one iteration.
- **Depends on.** A Windows PC now; self-hosted Windows and Mac runners later (shared with [E49](#e49)). [E54](#e54)'s event ring can share counters.
- **Done when.** A signed-off Windows checklist per feature with build and OS version. Fuzzers 1 h nightly with no crashes. pluginval strictness 10 passes on 3 OSes. An injected 1-sample skip in MixEngine is flagged. Nightly Linux and weekly Windows soaks report 0 discontinuities, 0 underruns, 0 NaN drops. The Windows loopback job captures a test tone within ±1 dB.
- **Evidence.** [CI workflow][CI] (jobs: core, sanitizers, rtsan, app; no soak, fuzz or pluginval); roadmap items 1.1, 1.10, 2.12 in [07][07]; [TRACEABILITY Known gaps][TR-gaps] R1.5, R4.4, R4.5; [PlatformServices_win.cpp][PSW] ~L869.

<a id="e06"></a>
#### E06 · SafetyGovernor redesign: measure all distortion, feed-forward control, weighted per-mode budgets, dynamics budget, govern base settings

*Audio quality · benefit 4 · difficulty Very high · slice 1–1.5 weeks, total 8–10 engineer-weeks · Ambitious*

- **Status (Phase 2 batch 1).** Slice partially done in `Protection.*`, `ProcessingChain.*` and `MeterBus.h`. (a) The governor ticks once per closed 10 ms window (dt = 10 ms, `SafetyGovernor::kTickMs` = `LoudnessMaximizer::kGrWindowMs`), and the chain splits each host block into segments that end on that grid, so a new scale takes effect at the same sample for any block size (ticking on the grid alone did not help: the scale still waited for the next host block); the module meters are combined over a block's segments. Boost 100 + Loudness 100 integrated-loudness spread over blocks 64–4096: pinned kick scene 0.25 → 0.047 LU (`KnownGap closed: governed macros at 64..4096-sample blocks`); CLI, 20 s: pink −18 dBFS 0.037 → 0.043, alternating sections 0.026 → 0.002, kicks 0.287 → 0.039 LU, all within the 0.05 LU Done-when. What is left is the saturator's and clipper's 25 ms THD+N windows, which close on the host's block boundaries below 480-sample blocks. (b) Protection strength Off (default) / Normal / Strict (`ProcessingChain::setProtectionStrength`, a host setting, no UI yet): Normal also scales the base `max.drive`, `sat.drive` and `bass.harmonics`, Strict does that with the scale's floor at 0 instead of 0.3 (`kMinScale` kept at Off / Normal). All Music macros 100 on −12 dBFS 50 Hz: 20.3 % THD+N at Off → 20.3 % at Normal (Music's base drives are 0 and the scale already sits at 0.3) and 5.3 % at Strict (6.0 % since [E04](#e04) took Tighten out of Punch; review re-base); with base `max.drive` 12 + `sat.drive` 12 Normal lowers the governor's own input −13.5 → −19.8 dB but the output's THD+N goes 17.2 → 19.7 % (18.1 → 19.8 % since E04), because less clipper drive hands the 50 Hz peaks to the limiter, whose distortion the governor does not measure: the ≤ 3 % Done-when is not met and needs step (1)'s residual fit and E05's LF envelope. (c) Scale, state (idle / backing off / holding / recovering), reason bits (limiter, distortion) and both ~3 s averages on `MeterBus` (`governorScale`, `governorState`, `governorReason`, `governorGrDb`, `governorDistortionDb`). Side effects: KnownGap pumping at 512-sample blocks re-baselined (spread 4.92 → 3.60 dB, time > 1 dB down 32.2 → 2.7 %; the metric depended on the host block before too, 3.76 dB / 3.2 % at 480), the Night Mode 10 s-event hole 3.25 → −0.31 dB (AutoLevel's upper-gate release count depended on the block; now the 64 / 480-sample value at every size); preset render diff against the pre-change build: every audio value within 0.05 dB, only `thdnMaxDb` moves (13 values, up to ±1.8 dB: the THD+N windows now close on the 10 ms grid), for which `tests/golden/preset-render-baseline.json` was re-recorded (every other value in it moved by at most 0.05 dB). Render cost +1–3 % (CLI, 60 s through Signature at 64 / 512 / 4096-sample blocks). Open: state and reason in the CLI `--json` and `BoostPanel`, the residual fit, feed-forward + PI, the Bark metric, PLR, the stationary sag / oscillation and 3-minute PLR / LRA measurements.
- **Status (Phase 2 batch 2, CLI).** Approach step (6)'s CLI half done in `tools/flubsound-cli/*`: `render.stats` carries the governor's scale, state and reasons at the end of the programme (`governor.end`, e.g. `backingOff` with `["limiter"]`) next to batch 1's time shares, and `--protection off|normal|strict` sets the strength for `process`, `batch` and `quality` (a host setting, not a `--set` key). Done-when "Scale and reason asserted in CLI `--json` tests" met (`tests/test_cli_stats.cpp`): −18 dBFS pink at Boost 100 + Loudness 100 with max.drive 24 and the clipper off ends backing off for the limiter (scale ≤ 0.6); a 50 Hz sine with every Music macro at 100 names the distortion budget; on this item's base-drive scene Normal lowers the measured THD+N below Off by ≥ 0.5 dB (−6.8 → −7.7 dB over 4 s) and Strict takes the scale below 0.3. The `BoostPanel` readout followed (app status line below).
- **Status (Phase 2 batch 2, step 1).** Approach step (1) done in `LoudnessMaximizer.*` and `ProcessingChain.cpp` ([03 §11.3.5](03-dsp-design.md#1135-thd-telemetry), [§14.5](03-dsp-design.md#145-protection-loops)): the maximizer's **whole-stage residual** (`getResidualDistortionDb()`), the least-squares THD+N of the limiter's output against the clipper's input (after the drive and the glue stage, whose all-pass split a scalar fit could not follow) delayed by the stage latency, over the same windows as the clipper's THD+N. It reads what the clipper's THD+N cannot, the limiter's gain modulation: limiter only at 12 dB drive, 50 + 63 Hz two-tone −35.7 dB and 55 Hz kicks under a 2 kHz tone −26.7 dB where the clipper reads −160 dB, pink −18 dBFS −29.7 dB (the E59 MTND on the same setting −28.3 dB), and equals an offline fit within 0.05 dB on every window. The governor takes it at protection strength **Normal and Strict** (the maximizer's share is the higher of it and the clipper's reading); Off keeps the clipper's reading, so the default sound and the preset renders do not move: the kicks scene through the limiter alone at base `max.drive` 12 stays at scale 1.00 at Off and goes to 0.53 at Normal, for the distortion budget. The clipper's and the residual's windows now close on the maximizer's own 10 ms grid (30 ms at 48 kHz) whatever the host block (they closed at the first block boundary at or after 25 ms): the chain's ragged-split test (Boost 100, clipper, governor acting) keeps its 1e−4 bound, which it missed (0.0127) with step 5's LF-first limiter while the windows still closed on segment boundaries, and the preset render diff moves only `thdnMaxDb` (7 values beyond 0.1 dB, at most +1.29 dB, Cinematic Adventure on tone-kicks). The Done-when scene (all Music macros 100 on 50 Hz) does not move at Normal (20.0 %): the limiter keeps its gain flat on a steady tone and the scale is at its 0.3 floor already; the output's THD+N is the bass harmonics generator's intended harmonics (its own reading −13.6 dB of the output against −14.0 dB THD+N, asserted), so ≤ 3 % at Normal needs Normal to govern the harmonics below the floor or count them against a budget (steps 3 / 5), not a better measure. With driven base settings: Off 18.4 → 22.5 % (Boost 100's LF-first limiter, [E05](#e05) step 5, lowers the 50 Hz fundamental while the harmonics above 120 Hz pass), Normal 19.9 % unchanged, Strict 9.3 → 9.6 %. Open: the harmonics policy for the ≤ 3 % row, the saturator's windows (still on segment boundaries, the remaining block-size dependence), feed-forward + PI, the Bark metric, PLR, `BoostPanel`.
- **Status (Phase 2 batch 2, app).** Step (6)'s UI half and the strength control done in `ui/BoostPanel.*`, `ui/SettingsDialog.*` and `EngineController`. Before: the Boost panel's chip said *Safety governor: NN% applied* (the scale only) and the app never set a protection strength (every chain ran at Off, no control). After: the chip reads *Governor 49% · limiter* (state and `kReason*` bits from `MeterBus`; *holding* / *recovering* / *limiter + distortion*), its tooltip gives the ~3 s limiter and distortion averages against the −6 / −30 dB budgets and the strength, and a click on it (or Settings › Processing › **Protection strength**) chooses Off / Normal / Strict. The strength is persisted (`protection.strength`), set on every strip at once (the chain's relaxed atomic) and re-applied to every engine the host builds (`onEngineConfigured` and the 2 Hz timer; a rebuilt engine's chains start at Off). End to end (`tests/app/test_app_ui_status.cpp`): the test programme at Boost 100 + Loudness 100, `max.drive` 24 dB, clipper off, 6 s: scale 0.49, backing off, reason limiter, limiter average −8.7 dB, chip *Governor 49% · limiter*. No sound moved at the default (Off); the offline export (`ExportJob`, not in this track's files) still renders at Off. Open: the residual-fit harmonics policy for the ≤ 3 % row, feed-forward + PI, the Bark metric, PLR.

- **Status (Phase 3, residual fit and weighted budgets).** Partially done: the Done-when row "all Music macros at 100 on 50 Hz: THD+N ≤ 3 % at Normal, unchanged at Off" is met, with the weighted whole-span measurement, feed-forward + PI control, a dynamics (PLR) budget and a harmonics policy, all at protection strength Normal / Strict (`Protection.*`, `ProcessingChain.*`, new `WeightedResidual.*`, `ParallelDistortion.h`; [03 §14.5](03-dsp-design.md#145-protection-loops) *The measured loop*). Off runs the stepwise loop and is bit-identical (nine CLI renders at 64 / 512 / 1000-sample blocks, the E59 quality suite 0 of 76 values moved on four settings, the preset render diff moves nothing). (a) **Measurement:** two spans, the saturator → maximizer ("drive") and the bass engine, each fitted per FFT bin against its latency-aligned input (the incoherent power is what no linear time-invariant response explains, the glue's all-pass split included), K-weighted, masked within each critical band by the programme's own coherent content with Johnston's tonality-dependent offset; guards keep subsonic leakage, slow gain riding and phase-locked harmonics from reading wrong (meta-validated: an LTI span −60.9 dB, a 1 % cubic within 0.03 / 0.09 dB of analytic at 55 Hz / 1 kHz, masked noise 25 dB under its flat reading, a 3 dB/s gain ramp −48 dB). (b) **Harmonics policy:** the bass harmonics never count against the drive; their share of the bass engine's audible residual drives a harmonics scale on `bass.harmonics` (a steady bass tone masks none of them, dense programme most), and Small Speaker Mode's harmonics, which replace the fundamental, are left alone at Normal (Strict governs them). (c) **Control:** the drive scale is the lowest of a limiter loop (a slow trim around a feed-forward from the maximizer input's 3 s peak statistics), the audible-residual loop and, in Music, a PLR loop (output PLR ≥ 8 dB and no more than 1 dB under the input's own); PI with a hold band and a probe memory against stages that switch in at a threshold; budgets −35 dB audible residual in Music, −30 dB in Gaming, Strict 6 dB lower (provisional, for the E60 panel); floors 0.3 (drive, Normal) / 0. Results at Normal, before → after: the 50 Hz scene **20.0 → 1.5 %** THD+N (Strict 6.0 → 1.6 %; with base `max.drive` 12 + `sat.drive` 12: 19.9 → 2.4 %, Strict 9.6 → 4.4 %; Off 20.0 / 22.5 % unchanged; `KnownGap closed: all Music macros at 100 on a 50 Hz sine ...`, one case per strength since the review). Stationary limiter-bound pink: settled by 3.5 s, then 0.08 dB of movement (the ≤ 0.2 dB oscillation row met); the settled level 0.6 dB under the first second, but a 4.3 dB transient dip around 2 s while the limiter's 800 ms programme envelope releases more slowly than the drive falls (the "sag ≤ 1 dB" row is not met by that reading). Loudness 100: audible residual −36.9 dB at 3 s (under the −35 dB budget: row met). E59 quality suite at Normal, Music Boost 100: THD+N 40 / 60 / 100 Hz −6.3 / −4.4 / −6.8 → −24.1 / −20.7 / −19.6 dB, bass IMD −8.0 → −26.9 dB, SMPTE −23.5 → −68.1 dB, MTND −10.2 / −11.8 / −10.1 → −18.0 / −20.9 / −24.8 dB, probe dip 3.0 → 0.1 dB; two readings worse because the suite's windows start 1 s into short renders, where a loop that is backing off still moves (maximizer alone at 12 dB drive, 40 Hz −124 → −23 dB, back at −128 dB after 5 s; Boost 100 at 1 kHz −95 → −41 dB). Block-size independence holds at Normal (480 / 4096 / ragged within 1e−4). Open: the 3-minute Boost 100 + Loudness 100 PLR ≥ 8 dB / LRA ≥ 6 LU row (the dynamics loop holds the 3 s PLR at ≥ 8.35 dB on 8 s of pink; not measured over 3 minutes of music); the transient dip (a drive fall rate tied to the limiter's release); publishing the harmonics scale, the span readings and the new `kReasonDynamics` / `kReasonHarmonics` on `MeterBus`, in `render.stats` and in the Boost panel's chip and tooltip (the tooltip still quotes the Off budgets at Normal); per-strip state across `reset()` and engine swaps; the E60 listening panel for the budgets.

- **Status (Phase 3 batch 2, sag, learned state, readouts).** Partially done: the stationary rows are met with the sag measured as a transient, the governor keeps its learned state across `reset()` and the engine swap, and its readouts are published; the 3-minute row is met on the E59 music-like programme but not on a mastered copy of it (`Protection.*`, `ProcessingChain.*`, `MeterBus.h`, `OfflineRenderer.*` and `Commands.*` of the CLI, one line in `MixEngine.cpp`; [03 §14.5](03-dsp-design.md#145-protection-loops) *The measured loop*). Off is bit-identical (36 CLI renders of four settings on three programmes at 64 / 512 / 1000-sample blocks, the preset render diff 125 of 125 renders at 0.0 dB tolerance, the 3-minute renders). (1) **Sag:** a release tie limits the drive's fall, in dB of drive, while the output is limiter-bound: by what the limiter's programme envelope (modelled on its window GR) has not yet taken while it attacks, plus what keeps the modelled dip (the falls not yet released) under 0.5 dB; the feed-forward now models that envelope and the clipper, predicts from 80 ms of programme (was 0.5 s) at the loop's set point, and its share uses the drive before `max.drive`'s clamp (it read 3 dB high on the batch 1 scene); the limiter loop yields while the limiter is idle. Stationary limiter-bound pink (the batch 1 scene): the deepest 0.5 s window from 0.5 s on under the settled level **4.29 → 0.61 dB** (Done-when sag ≤ 1 dB met), the scale within 0.01 dB of its final value at 3 s (was 3.5 s; settled row met) and moving 0.12 dB after 4 s (≤ 0.2 dB row met); on nine more stationary settings (seeds, level, base drive, clipper, Music) 1.3–4.7 → 0.39–0.92 dB. Loudness 100: audible residual −36.8 dB at 3 s (budget −35, row met), output PLR 8.40 dB at 8 s. (2) **3 minutes** of the E59 music-like programme (`makeMusic`, twelve 15 s sections at 0 … −14 dB: −20.5 LUFS, LRA 12.3 LU, PLR 19.8 dB) at Music Boost 100 + Loudness 100 through the CLI: Normal **LRA 8.42 LU, PLR 15.5 dB** (row met; Off 2.95 / 11.1, Strict 11.8 / 18.9). Not met on the same programme first mastered (LRA 8.5 LU, PLR 13.4 dB): Normal keeps 3.95 LU (PLR 12.1 dB; Off 1.29, Strict 6.58 LU): the drive scale sits at its 0.3 floor 97 % of the time for the audible residual (−25 dB mean against −35), dominated by the bass engine's non-harmonic share (about −29 dB, the drive span −36 dB), which the floor cannot bring under the budget. (3) **Readouts:** `MeterBus::governorStrength`, `governorHarmonicsScale`, `governorTonalScale`, `governorDriveResidualDb` (what the drive loop compares), `governorHarmonicsResidualDb`, `governorBassResidualDb`, `governorPlrDb`, the budgets of the mode (`governorResidualBudgetDb`, `governorGrBudgetDb`, `governorPlrBudgetDb`) and the brightness lifts with their budgets (`tonalLiftDb`, `tonalBudgetDb`), with `kReasonDynamics` / `kReasonHarmonics` / `kReasonTonal` in `governorReason`; `render.stats.governor.measured` (scales min / end, residuals max / mean / end, PLR min / end, brightness max / end, the time each new reason held) and `governor.end.reasons` naming `dynamics` / `harmonics` / `tonal`; `flubsound-cli quality` adds the pink render's governor (`loudness.pinkGovernor`, a *Governor* line). The app shows them since Phase 3 batch 2 ([E38](#e38)'s app line: the loudness panel's PROTECTION section and the governor chip). (4) **Learned state:** `SafetyGovernor::Memory` (the three scales, each loop's probe memory) survives a chain's `reset()` at Normal / Strict (held until the readings are back): on the batch 1 scene a reset at 6 s left the scale at −8.36 → at most −8.17 dB and the limiter's deepest GR −5.5 dB (batch 1 went back to scale 1 and −15 to −18 dB of GR); `MixEngine::configureFrom` hands each strip's strength and learned state to the new engine (−8.55 dB running, the new engine at most −8.44 dB in its first 0.5 s, 0 dB without the hand-over; Off's stepwise scale too). Moves at Normal: the E06 KnownGap cases and every other KnownGap value unchanged; E59 quality suite at Normal mostly better (Boost 100 + Loudness 100 1 kHz THD+N −29.1 → −37.4 dB, probe lift 4.5–5.0 → 3.2 dB; maximizer alone at 12 dB 100 Hz / 1 kHz −40.7 / −40.5 → −62.4 / −110.3 dB), worse at 60 Hz with the maximizer alone at 12 dB (−39.3 → −35.7 dB) and its two-tone (−47.2 → −46.0 dB). Tests: `tests/test_protection_readouts.cpp` (new), the Done-when and feed-forward cases in `tests/test_protection_measured.cpp`. Open: the mastered-music LRA (the bass engine's non-harmonic residual against the budget, or a budget from the E60 panel), the E60 listening panel for the budgets. *Review (Phase 3 batch 2):* with [E14](#e14)'s Warmth remap the Done-when scene (all Music macros 100, 50 Hz) failed its ≤ 3 % row at Normal (−36.28 → −25.99 dB): the loop reaches its 0.3 floor by 3 s and then probes (recovers at 1 dB/s from about 7 s, backs off near 10 s), and the single sine fit over 6 – 10 s counts that ramp as THD+N (the v1 Warmth passed only because its probe fell outside the window: −28.1 dB over 2 – 6 s). The Normal / Strict rows now read the THD+N with the level drift tracked (a sine fit per 100 ms), as a tracking-notch analyser does: Normal −41.7 dB (0.8 %; driven −38.7 dB), Strict −52.3 / −50.6 dB; the plain values stay printed; Off re-pinned for E14 (−13.96 / −12.96 / −13.58 → −16.99 / −17.21 / −16.11 dB). The probing on a steady tone is the probe memory as designed; the "holds still" row is measured on the stationary limiter-bound scene.

- **Status (p3b3, app).** The governor chip's tooltip reads the per-mode budgets the chain publishes on `MeterBus` (since Phase 3 batch 2, [E38](#e38)'s app line; the remark above that it *still quotes the Off budgets at Normal* is out of date), and now quotes them before the first reading as well (*not measured yet (budget −41 dB)*, where it said *not measured*). The loudness panel's Distortion row marks Off's −30 dB THD+N budget only at Off, the only strength that budgets it (Normal / Strict budget the audible residual, the PROTECTION section's row). End to end (`tests/app/test_app_ui_status.cpp`): the Music / Game strips at Normal quote −35 / −30 dB of residual and −6 dB of limiter budget, at Strict −41 / −36 dB and −4 dB (at Off −6 dB and THD+N −30 dB). No sound, golden or parameter moves. Open as above: the mastered-music LRA, the E60 listening panel for the budgets.
- **Status (p3b5, prepare keeps the learned state).** Done in `Protection.*` (`SafetyGovernor::prepare (rate, keepLearned)`) and `ProcessingChain::prepare()`: a re-prepare of the same chain at the same sample rate and input layout (a plug-in host's `prepareToPlay` for a new block size or a transport start) keeps what the governor has learned, as `reset()` does (Normal / Strict; at Off exactly a reset, as before); a new rate or layout is a new device and starts afresh. Before → after on the limiter-bound scene at Normal: learned −8.35 dB, then a re-prepare at 256-sample blocks started again from 0 dB → holds at most −8.17 dB in the 250 ms after; a 5.1 re-prepare and a new rate start from 0 dB (`tests/test_protection_measured.cpp`, two cases). Nothing moves at Off, and no render prepares a chain twice: no golden, render or KnownGap value moves. Open as above: the mastered-music LRA, the E60 listening panel for the budgets.
- **What.** (1) Measure the whole maximizer's nonlinearity (glue + clipper + limiter gain-modulation IMD) with a least-squares fit of output against the latency-aligned input. (2) Replace the slow asymmetric 3 s loop with feed-forward from pre-maximizer loudness and crest factor plus a PI trim with anti-windup, keeping per-strip state across `reset()` and engine swaps. (3) A masking-weighted metric (about 20 Bark bands) with per-mode budgets (Audiophile strict, Club lenient, Music about −40 dB). (4) A Music dynamics budget based on PLR / short-term crest. (5) Govern base `max.drive`, `sat.drive` and harmonics behind a visible "protection strength" control (Off / Normal / Strict). (6) Publish scale and reason in the UI and CLI JSON.
- **Why.** Music: loud settings hold audible IMD with no back-off (24 s measured), loudness sags and hunts, and Boost + Loudness cuts LRA from 11.4 to 1.4 LU for +2 LU of loudness. Gaming: level breathing and uncontrolled distortion at high Boost.
- **Expected benefit.** Closes the open part of R3.5 (base drive "measured but not limited"). Today: Loudness 100 gives −21.8 dB MTND with no back-off for 24 s; stationary programme sags 4.7 dB in 5 s and oscillates until 45 s; Music with all macros at 100 on 50 Hz gives 27.6 % THD+N; everything maxed gives −2.38 LUFS as a near-square wave. The skeptic re-measured block-size dependence as 0.22 LU (not 0.71) and traced it to the governor loop, not the maximizer (fixed drive gives −6.72 LUFS at every block size). **`d096a4d` did not close this gap**, although its title claims "governor block-size independence": it moved the governor's limiter-GR input to a fixed 10 ms window, but the loop still runs per host block with a hard over/under decision. On a build containing `d096a4d`, Boost 100 + Loudness 100 still spreads 0.35 LU over blocks 64–4096 on the post-report review's stimulus (−7.59 to −7.94 LUFS) and 0.08 LU on a 20 s −18 dBFS-RMS pink (−7.30 to −7.38 LUFS), with output identical to the round-7 build in both cases. The spread depends on the stimulus (0.08 / 0.22 / 0.35 LU), and every value misses the 0.05 LU target below.
- **Approach.** Correction to the original diagnosis: the maximizer already publishes a grid-aligned window GR (`getWindowGainReductionDb()`), and the governor consumes it. The remaining dependence comes from `SafetyGovernor::update` running per host block with a hard over/under decision and an `exp(-dt/3)` average. Before `d096a4d` that average was fed a stale window value; since `d096a4d` it receives the deepest GR per fixed 10 ms window ([Protection.h][PRh] header comment), but `update()` is still called once per host block with dt = block length and still steps `scale` by `kFallPerSec * dt` / `kRisePerSec * dt` on a binary decision, so the scale trajectory still follows the block grid (measured above). Slice: tick the governor only when a window closes (dt = window length); add a base-drive path behind the strength control; revisit `kMinScale 0.3`; publish scale and reason via `MeterBus` and `--json`. Later: residual fit inside `LoudnessMaximizer`; feed-forward + PI; Bark metric from `Fft.cpp` every 50–100 ms; PLR instead of LRA (LRA is a multi-minute statistic); reason shown in `BoostPanel`.
- **Depends on.** Slice: nothing; land it before [E05](#e05). The residual fit follows E05's structure. The in-flight engine swap must carry governor state. AutoLevel convergence is unverified (R3.2).
- **Done when.** Block sizes 64–4096: integrated LUFS spread ≤ 0.05 LU with governed macros. Stationary programme: sag ≤ 1 dB, settled within 3 s, oscillation ≤ 0.2 dB. Loudness 100: residual under budget within 3 s. Boost 100 + Loudness 100 over 3 minutes: PLR ≥ 8 dB, LRA ≥ 6 LU. All Music macros at 100 on 50 Hz: THD+N ≤ 3 % at Normal, unchanged at Off. Scale and reason asserted in CLI `--json` tests.
- **Evidence.** [Protection.cpp][PR] `SafetyGovernor::update(limiterGrDb, distortionDb)`, `kMinScale`; [Protection.h][PRh] budgets −6 dB GR / −30 dB THD+N; [LoudnessMaximizer.cpp][LM] window GR; [MacroMap.cpp][MM] `governed` flags; [TRACEABILITY Known gaps][TR-gaps] R3.5.

<a id="e07"></a>
#### E07 · High-frequency harshness control: post-enhancement de-ess/de-harsh guard, level-relative presence, tonal-balance governor

*Audio quality · benefit 4 · difficulty High · 5–6 engineer-weeks*

- **Status (Phase 3).** Partially done in new `SmoothnessGuard.*` and `TonalBalanceMeter.*`, `Protection.*`, `ProcessingChain.*` and `Parameters.*` ([03 §14.5](03-dsp-design.md#145-protection-loops) *High-frequency harshness*); off by default, so no factory preset render moves (125 of 125 identical at 0.0 dB tolerance). (a) **Smoothness** (`smooth.amount`, layout version 4, default 0): a new slot `SSmooth` after `SSat`, before the spatializer, compressor and maximizer, zero latency. It de-esses against a reference instead of a threshold: the 5 – 10 kHz band over the 150 Hz – 2 kHz body of its input against the same ratio at the dynamic EQ's input (delayed by the slots in between), cut where the input is hotter than the reference and the band at least 0 dB over its body (+3 dB in Gaming, whose light guard caps the cut at 6 dB against Music's 9 dB). So it takes back what the enhancement added, at any level, and never de-esses the source; its depth follows the macros because the lift it removes is theirs. Sung-vocal stand-in, sibilance / voice against bypass, Smoothness 0 → 100: Boost 100 + Clarity 100 **+2.68 → +1.24 dB** at 0 dB input and +3.39 → +1.69 dB at +8 dB (rows not met); Boost 70 + Clarity 70 +2.06 → +0.56 dB and +2.84 → +0.95 dB; Clarity 100 alone +1.73 → +0.15 dB (these are CLI renders read at 8–12 s; the pinned `KnownGap: sibilance over voice ...` case, on its own shorter stimulus, reads +2.64 → +1.17 / +3.61 → +2.00 dB at Boost 100 + Clarity 100, +0.50 at Boost 70 + Clarity 70, +0.12 for Clarity 100 and +0.28 dB at −10 dB input with Smoothness 100). What is left at Boost 100 is the maximizer after the stage, which ducks the loud vowels 0.6 dB more than the quieter "s" (at −10 dB input +2.81 → +0.41 dB). Smoothness 0 is bit-exact (row met). (b) **Tonal-balance rule** at protection strength Normal / Strict, on E06's governor: `TonalBalanceMeter` compares the dynamic EQ's input with the output (presence 2 – 5, harsh 5 – 10, air 10 – 16 kHz lifts over the 200 Hz – 1 kHz lift, 1 s averages) and a third PI loop scales what the macros add to `clarity.presence` and `clarity.air` and the Gaming Voice & Score and Music air band ranges (never base values, the user's EQ, the cue bands or the drive) while a band is over budget (presence +2 dB Gaming, +3 dB Music; Strict 1.5 dB lower); `kReasonTonal`. Gaming full stack on −30 dBFS pink, 2 – 5 kHz lift minus 200 Hz – 1 kHz lift, at Normal **+4.75 → +1.28 dB** (Strict +0.67; row met at Normal / Strict; Off +4.57, unchanged; CLI renders at 8–12 s; the `KnownGap closed at protection strength Normal: Gaming full stack ...` case reads Off +4.06 → Normal +1.26 dB, 5 – 10 kHz +1.66 → +0.67 dB); Music Boost 100 + Clarity 100 on pink, relative presence at −45 against −12 dBFS 6.65 → 2.95 dB at Normal (the KnownGap case: 6.67 → 3.17 dB). Footstep steps under a bed (Footsteps 100 + Boost 100) keep their lift with Smoothness 100 at Normal (13.50 → 13.10 dB); *Factory presets: … gaming presets keep positional cues* passes. Cost: a Normal render +12 % (60 s stereo, 4.10 → 4.61 s), Smoothness +3 %. Open: the sibilance rows at Boost 100 (the stage cannot see the maximizer after it); the presence row for base values (clarity.presence 1 still lifts 4.86 dB more at −45 than at −12 dBFS, 5.21 dB in the KnownGap case: approach step 3, the level-relative presence in `ClarityEnhancer.cpp`, not in this change); moving the Music de-harsh mode band out of the dynamic EQ (kept: moving it would re-voice Music at Smoothness 0); the brightness meter on `MeterBus`, in `render.stats` and in the UI (the chain publishes `getTonalLiftDb`, `getGovernorTonalScale` and `getSmoothnessCutDb`); Smoothness in factory presets (a product decision); the upward compressor's lifts are not in the rule; the 30-minute fatigue session (E60).
- **Status (Phase 3 batch 2, app).** Smoothness has app controls: a **Smoothness** slider (0–100 %) beside Dynamic Range under the macros of the Simple view ([06 §3.5](06-gui.md#35-simple-view)) and a *Smooth* key on the Clarity card of the Advanced view's rack (its layout group; the stage runs with Clarity off too, so the key is not dimmed with the module; `ModuleDescriptor::Key::independent`). Before: only in the expanded Clarity card's generic grid. Both write the selected strip's `smooth.amount` (default 0, so no sound changed); `tests/app/test_app_ui_guards.cpp`. The rest of the Phase 3 line is open as listed there (the brightness meter in the UI among it).
- **Status (Phase 3 batch 2, app readouts).** The brightness meter is in the UI: the loudness panel's PROTECTION section and the governor chip's tooltip show the presence / harsh / air lifts against their budgets at Normal / Strict ([E38](#e38)'s batch 2 line).
- **Status (Phase 3 batch 2, sibilance at Boost 100 and readouts).** Partially done in `SmoothnessGuard.*`, `ProcessingChain.*`, `MeterBus.h` and the CLI's `render.stats` ([03 §14.5](03-dsp-design.md#145-protection-loops) *Output coupling*). (1) **Sibilance rows at Boost 100 met** (with Smoothness 100): the chain hands the stage its output (after the maximizer, before the output gain), and the stage holds each "s" to the voice's lift there instead of to the balance under the "s" at its input: excess = min (ratio − threshold, sibilant-band lift at the stage + what the slots after it do to that band now − the body's 300 ms lift at the output). What stayed was the maximizer ducking the loud vowels 0.6 dB more than the "s" and the inverse-level lifts raising the body under an "s" more than the vowel; the stage now reads both. Before → after, the pinned case (renamed *KnownGap closed: sibilance over voice ...*): Boost 100 + Clarity 100 at 0 dB input **+1.18 → +0.25 dB** (row ≤ +0.5 met), at +8 dB **+2.01 → +0.44 dB** (row ≤ +1 met), at −10 dB +0.28 → +0.06 dB, Boost 70 + Clarity 70 +0.50 → +0.18 dB, Clarity 100 alone +0.12 → +0.15 dB; CLI renders (6 s, read as in batch 1) +1.24 → +0.32 / +1.69 → +0.57 dB, Boost 70 + Clarity 70 +0.56 → +0.25 / +0.95 → +0.49 dB, Clarity 100 +0.15 → +0.20 dB. Smoothness 0 stays bit-exact, so no factory preset moves (preset render diff 125 of 125 identical at 0.0 dB tolerance against the build before), and every other measured value of the whole suite is unchanged (the footstep lift at Normal with Smoothness 100 13.10 dB, the tonal rows). Render cost unchanged within noise (Smoothness on about +2–3 % of a render). (2) **Readouts:** the brightness lifts and budgets were already on `MeterBus` (`tonalLiftDb`, `tonalBudgetDb`), in `render.stats.governor.measured` and in the app's LoudnessPanel (E06 batch 2 and the app track); new: the Smoothness cut on `MeterBus::smoothnessCutDb` and `render.stats.smoothness` (`cutMaxDb`, `activePercent`). The brightness is measured only at protection strength Normal / Strict (its meter costs about 12 % of a render). Tests: *Smoothness: handed the chain's output ...* (a limiter model after the stage: +1.68 → +0.45 dB) in `tests/test_protection_tonal.cpp`, the MeterBus check in *Chain: Smoothness 0 is bit-exact ...*, *CLI: render.stats carries the Smoothness stage's cut ...* in `tests/test_protection_readouts.cpp`. Not done: the presence row for base values (`clarity.presence` 1 still lifts 5.21 dB more at −45 than at −12 dBFS; Music Boost 100 + Clarity 100 at Normal 3.17 dB): it needs approach step 3 in `ClarityEnhancer.cpp` (the presence's absolute −18 dB threshold in `presenceGainDb` replaced by band followers relative to the programme's balance), outside this track's files; the chain can only scale the presence down, which would not lift loud masters. Open as before: the app display of the Smoothness cut, Smoothness in factory presets (a product decision), the Music de-harsh band's move, the upward compressor's lifts in the rule, the 30-minute fatigue session (E60).
- **Status (p3b3, app).** The app display of the Smoothness cut is done: the Clarity card's note reads *Smoothness cut −3.2 dB (5 - 10 kHz)* while the stage cuts (`ProcessingChain::getSmoothnessCutDb`, shown from −0.05 dB and repainted in 0.1 dB steps; `ui/ModuleCard.*`, `ui/ModuleRack.*`). Tested with white noise through the Music chain at Boost and Clarity 100 with Smoothness 100, the card showing the chain's cut (`tests/app/test_app_ui_guards.cpp`); the screenshot scene's test programme is too dark for the stage to cut, so no screenshot shows it. No sound moves. Open as before: the presence row for base values, Smoothness in factory presets (a product decision), the Music de-harsh band's move, the upward compressor's lifts in the rule, the 30-minute fatigue session (E60).
- **Status (p3b4, level-relative presence and the upward lifts in the tonal rule).** Partially done in `ClarityEnhancer.*`, `TonalBalanceMeter.*`, `Parameters.*` and `ProcessingChain.cpp` ([03 §5.3.3](03-dsp-design.md#533-dynamic-presence-inverse-level-boost), [§14.5](03-dsp-design.md#145-protection-loops)). (1) **Approach step 3:** `clarity.presenceMode` (layout version 7, Absolute / Relative, **Absolute by default and bit-identical**: preset render diff 125 of 125 renders identical at 0.0 dB tolerance against the build before). Relative reads the presence band (the bell's Q 0.8 detector, about 2 – 5 kHz at 3.2 kHz) against the programme's own 200 Hz – 1 kHz body: the larger of the balance of 1 s followers and of the 20 ms detectors, boost = (8 dB − balance) / 4, 0 … 6 dB, the same −80 dB noise-floor taper; the threshold is set so pink gets what Absolute gives it at −18 dBFS RMS (AutoLevel's default target: 1.94 against 1.99 dB). A switch crossfades the two laws over 20 ms after a 60 ms warm-up of the followers (click test ≤ −80 dBFS, block-size bit-exact); in the app it is in the expanded Clarity card's generic grid, with a hint (`ui/ParamHints.cpp`). Done-when rows, before (Absolute) → after (Relative): `clarity.presence` 1, 2.5 – 4 kHz lift at −45 minus −12 dBFS **5.21 → 0.00 dB** (row met; *KnownGap closed with clarity.presenceMode Relative ...*); Music Boost 100 + Clarity 100 at Normal **3.17 → 2.00 dB** (row not met, KnownGap: what is left is the Clarity macro's de-harsh mode band, a CutAbove over a fixed −22 dB in `configureModeBands` that cuts −12 dBFS pink and not −45 dBFS pink: Clarity 100 alone reads 2.2 dB with Relative presence, Boost 100 alone −0.5 dB; it needs approach step 1, outside this unit's files). Making Relative the default re-voices every preset that lifts presence (all Boost settings): an owner decision, with the render diff recorded here as its before. (2) **The upward lifts in the tonal rule:** `TonalBalanceMeter` divides each 10 ms window of both sides by the programme's level (the reference over the bands, 50 ms, floored at −70 dB), so a quiet passage weighs as much as a loud one and the lifts of quiet passages - the upward compressor's, the inverse-level presence's, the boost-below bands' - count; a steady programme reads as before. Before → after: a +6 dB bell on the −45 dBFS passages of pink alternating with −12 dBFS passages 0.01 → 1.92 dB; an 8 dB upward lift of bright −45 dBFS passages between dark −12 dBFS ones 0.22 → 3.90 dB; through the chain (Music Boost 100 + Clarity 100 on those passages) the tonal scale at Normal 1.000 → 0.891 (Strict 0.651 → 0.380) and the quiet passages' presence lift 3.28 → 2.95 dB (Strict 2.20 → 1.50; Off 3.36). The Gaming full-stack row is unchanged at Normal (1.26 dB, ≤ +2 dB met). Only protection strength Normal / Strict moves, by hundredths on steady programme: the 50 Hz scene at Normal −25.99 → −26.02 dB, with `max.drive 12` + `sat.drive 12` at Strict −23.06 → −22.93 dB (governor scale 0.06 → 0.07, harmonics share −56.93 → −56.13 dB), the footstep lift at Normal 13.10 → 13.11 dB; no golden render, render baseline or quality-target row moves (all at Off). `parameter-defaults.json` gains the key and every factory `contentHash` moves (re-recorded once in the p3b4 review: `clarity.presenceMode` = 0 in `parameter-defaults.json`, the 25 existing `contentHash` values in `factory-presets.json`; no golden render moved by this unit). Tests: three *Clarity (E07 step 3): ...* cases in `tests/test_clarity.cpp`, *TonalBalanceMeter (E07): every passage weighs the same ...* and *Chain (E07): at protection strength Normal the tonal rule sees ...* in `tests/test_protection_tonal.cpp`, the two presence cases and E59's presence-invariance row in `tests/test_known_gaps.cpp`. Open: the Music de-harsh band's move (approach step 1, now also the presence row's remainder) and the Gaming Voice & Score band's fixed −36 dB threshold (E59 row: 1.46 dB with Relative); Relative as the default and Smoothness in factory presets (owner decisions); the 30-minute fatigue session (E60).
- **Status (p3b5, app card).** `clarity.presenceMode` is on the Clarity card of the module rack (a choice, *Presence Mode*), not only in the expanded card's grid (`ui/ModuleCard.cpp` table; `tests/app/test_app_ui_guards.cpp`, screenshot state `module-keys`). No sound moves.
- **What.** A "Smoothness" de-esser/de-harsh stage (split-band detection over 4.5–10 kHz, threshold relative to broadband) in a second, post-enhancement `DynamicEq` instance after Clarity and the Saturator and before the Compressor and Maximizer, with depth scaled by Clarity, Boost and Detail. Presence becomes relative to programme balance (3 kHz against 200 Hz–1 kHz and 6–10 kHz) with 2–3 centre choices. A tonal-balance guard in Protection tracks the long-term relative 1/3-octave spectrum and scales back ungoverned presence, air and upward lifts, with a brightness meter. Gaming gets only a light de-harsh guard.
- **Why.** Music: Boost + Clarity raises sibilance, loud masters get no presence while quiet ones get +6 dB, and the result is fatiguing, the classic complaint against FxSound/Boom-style enhancers. Gaming: the detail stack lifts 2–5 kHz on beds and nothing reacts.
- **Expected benefit.** Stops sibilance/voice ratio rising (+2.1 dB at `-b 100`, +3.6 dB with Clarity 100, +4.6 dB at +8 dB input; a 7 kHz CutAbove band reverses it to −1.9 dB) and makes presence independent of mastering level (+5.7 dB at −45 dBFS against +0.9 dB at −12 dBFS). The gaming evidence is overstated: on −30 dBFS pink with Gaming, Boost 100 and all macros 100, the 2–5 kHz tilt against the mids is only +4.0 dB; most of the "+12.2 dB" is level, not brightness. "Presence ungoverned" is a documented design choice, not a TRACEABILITY gap.
- **Approach.** (1) A new slot after `SSat` in `SlotIndex`; move the Music de-harsh mode band out of the pre-enhancement DynEq (`configureModeBands`). The upward compressor in `SComp` still follows, so detection must be relative. (2) Relative detection as the de-mud stage already does (`kDeMudRelativeDb`), with new governed MacroMap entries. (3) Replace `kPresenceThresholdDb −18` in `presenceGainDb()` with band followers; ship together with (1). (4) The tonal guard acts only on ungoverned macro lifts (presence, air, M5 voice band), never on the user's parametric EQ. (5) Brightness meter in `MeterBus`.
- **Depends on.** The in-flight engine swap (a new slot changes `kNumSlots`). [E06](#e06)'s governor framework. [E04](#e04)'s HF band and [E08](#e08)'s air rebuild add brightness the guard must see.
- **Done when.** Sung vocal at Boost 100 + Clarity 100: sibilance/voice ≤ +0.5 dB against bypass (≤ +1 dB at +8 dB input). Pink at −45 and −12 dBFS: presence lifts within 1.5 dB. Gaming full stack: 2–5 kHz lift minus 200 Hz–1 kHz lift ≤ +2 dB (today +4.0). The "gaming presets keep positional cues" test passes. Smoothness 0 is bit-exact. A 30-minute fatigue session on bright pop/rock at Boost 70 + Clarity 70.
- **Evidence.** [ProcessingChain.h][PCh] `SlotIndex`; [ProcessingChain.cpp][PC] `configureModeBands` (Music de-harsh is a single 3.5 kHz bell before brightening); [ClarityEnhancer.cpp][CE] `presenceGainDb`, `kPresenceThresholdDb`; [MacroMap.cpp][MM] `governed=false` on presence and air.

<a id="e10"></a>
#### E10 · Signal hygiene: rate-aware anti-aliasing, ultrasonic band-limiting, DC blocker, and garbage-input sanitising

*Audio quality · benefit 4 · difficulty Medium · Phase 1 4–6 days, total 4–6 engineer-weeks*

- **Status (Phase 2 batch 1).** Phase 1 partially done. (1) Residual-path DC high-pass (5 Hz, 1st-order TPT, double state) on the maximizer clipper's correction in `LoudnessMaximizer.*`: 100 + 200 Hz at max.drive 24, output DC −15.8 dBFS in every profile → −73.6 (Quality), −68.8 (Balanced), −58.7 dBFS (Low Latency); the Low Latency miss (Done-when ≤ −60) and −51.4 / −49.0 / −42.2 dBFS with the clipper off came from the true-peak limiter's gain modulation, which a residual filter cannot touch without moving peaks past the ceiling; [E05](#e05) stage 1's period hold keeps that gain flat from peak to peak, so all six values are now about −141 dBFS (`KnownGap closed: DC after the maximizer`). The saturator (Tube keeps its 10 Hz blocker), bass harmonics and air exciter were not changed. (2) Input sanitiser in `ProcessingChain.*` / `Protection.*`: a finite sample beyond +24 dBFS is muted (not clamped) and counted, and its block is hidden from AutoLevel, AutoDrive, the governor's detectors and the loudness match (they hold); counters `MeterBus::corruptSampleCount` / `droppedBlockCount`. One 1e30 sample in −20 dBFS pink through Signature: difference above −60 dBFS 4003 ms (to the end of the render), worst level change after 100 ms +12.0 dB → 7 ms and 0.00 dB (−1e30 on both channels the same); a 10 ms NaN burst unchanged at 800 ms / 0.26 dB, 441 ms / 0.06 dB since [E12](#e12) Phase A's width guard (`KnownGap closed: a single 1e30 sample ...`). Open: `DriftCompensatedFifo::push` sanitising and the PipeWire note, the other residual paths, steps (3)–(4) (rate-aware oversampling table, `tests/test_signal_hygiene.cpp`) and the alias, ultrasonic and realtime-factor Done-when rows.
- **Status (Phase 2 batch 2).** Phase 1 steps (1)–(4) done for the saturator, the capture FIFO and the measurements; the alias rows met at Warmth 100, not at harder drive. (1) Residual-path DC blocker (5 Hz, 1st-order TPT, double state) on the saturator's band-limited deviation (`Saturator.*`): 0.35 sin 100 Hz + 0.35 cos 200 Hz at 48 kHz, DC at 12 / 24 dB drive: Tape −29.8 / −36.2 → below −150 dBFS, Digital −28.2 / −35.9 → below −150 dBFS (Tube's 10 Hz blocker stays; it already read −150). The bass harmonics generator (HP2 at its cutoff) and the air exciter (HP4 at 7 kHz) already high-pass what they shape: below −150 dBFS with even harmonics at full amount, no change needed. (2) `DriftCompensatedFifo::push` mutes and counts (`Stats::corruptSamples`) NaN / Inf and beyond-+24 dBFS samples per stream, a surround push per source sample before the downmix; a corrupted stream and the same stream with those samples zeroed give bit-identical output. The PipeWire note is in [03 §0.11](03-dsp-design.md#011-denormal-and-non-finite-policy) and the FIFO header. (3) Rate-aware table `Oversampler::forProfile()` (`Oversampler.*`: a stage-1 `DecimatorStage` whose transition ends near the image of 20 kHz; `ProcessingChain::prepare` calls it, one line per profile): below 176.4 kHz 4× in the latency of the old 2× designs (Quality 32, Balanced / Low Latency 16 samples, so no chain latency changes), 2× High / Low from 176.4 kHz. This differs from the plan (2× at 88.2 / 96 kHz, 1× + LP22k from 176.4 kHz): at 96 kHz 4× is what removes the harmonics that fold inside the 2× domain (0 dBFS Tape tone −46.1 → −88.6 dBc), and a 1× residual low-pass aliased (−46.1 dBc) and could not reach 22 kHz in 16 samples. Worst in-band alias of −6 dBFS 1 / 5 / 7 / 10 kHz sines at Warmth 100 (Tape, 9 dB), saturator, Quality / Balanced and Low Latency: 44.1 kHz −32.2 → −78.5 / −32.3 → −74.2 dBc, 48 kHz −41.2 → −83.2 / −41.1 → −78.0, 96 kHz −83.1 → −132.1 / −83.1 → −100.0, 192 kHz unchanged (−148.9 / −118.1); whole chain (Music, Warmth 100): 44.1 kHz −36.6 → −91.0 / −36.6 → −74.7 dBc, 48 kHz −46.9 → −96.5 / −46.7 → −79.9, 96 kHz −92.3 → −102.3 / −92.0 → −102.2 (Done-when met at this setting). Realtime factor with Warmth 100 (CLI, 20 s of pink noise): −14 % (Balanced) / −22 % (Quality) at 48 kHz, −18 / −23 % at 96 kHz (≤ 25 % met); unchanged with the saturator off. (4) `tests/test_signal_hygiene.cpp` (8 cases: the table's latency, the alias rows, the chain row, a KnownGap ratchet of the unmet rows, the DC blockers, the FIFO sanitiser) and the hygiene family of `flubsound-cli quality --rate R` (alias, DC, ultrasonic share), with nightly rows over 3 profiles × 4 rates in `tests/quality_targets.json` ([E59](#e59)). Side effects: preset render diff against the pre-change build: only the two presets with `sat.on` move, Warm Vinyl and Lo-Fi Chill: `thdnMaxDb` −35.73 → −36.25 dB (Warm Vinyl, ambush: the saturator's THD+N is now measured at 4×), true peak up to +0.08 dB, every other value within 0.03 dB; their golden renders (`tests/golden/factory-presets.json`, reference leg) moved past the 0.05 dB gate in the 20 kHz band, Warm Vinyl −40.57 → −40.41 dB and Lo-Fi Chill −63.41 → −63.33 dB (integrated +0.009 / +0.004 LU, 16 kHz and 25–63 Hz bands ≤ 0.05 dB), re-recorded in review; KnownGap tests: only the E06 strength rows with `sat.drive` move (50 Hz THD+N by at most 0.34 %, governor THD+N input up to +0.18 dB), all pass. Open: harder drive at 44.1 kHz (24 dB: Tape −28.0, Digital −35.5 dBc; a 0 dBFS sine at 9 dB −45.8 dBc; pinned as KnownGap) needs 8× or ADAA (step 3's Low Latency ADAA is not done); every Music macro at 100 at 44.1 kHz reads −34.4 dBc (the air exciter feeding the saturator; the exciter alone −63.0 dBc at 10 kHz, [E08](#e08)); at 192 kHz the 22–96 kHz energy is −69.8 dB at Warmth 100 but −55.5 dB with every macro (no LP22k on the residuals at 88.2 kHz and above); the maximizer clipper's oversampling is not in the table; a symmetric / polyphase decimator would cut its CPU.
- **Status (Phase 2 batch 2, chain).** DC in Low Latency and after the maximizer's limiter: closed by [E05](#e05) stage 1's period hold, re-measured on this tree (`KnownGap closed: DC after the maximizer`, 100 + 200 Hz at 24 dB drive, chain / limiter alone: Quality −142.0 / −141.7, Balanced −142.0 / −141.7, Low Latency −141.2 / −141.8 dBFS). The chain's other limiter, the bypass reference's (`dryLimiter`, [03 §14.5](03-dsp-design.md#145-protection-loops)), kept the plain envelope: 100 + 200 Hz at +1.5 dBFS peak in bypass −55.7 dBFS DC (over the −60 dBFS Done-when) and a +2.9 dBFS 50 Hz tone −39.2 dB THD+N. It now has the LF-safe envelope: −176.7 dBFS DC, −147.7 dB THD+N, peak −1.05 dBFS unchanged, unity while idle (test *Chain: the bypass reference of hot bass is limited without DC or THD*). Open: the MixEngine master limiter still has the plain envelope (`MixEngine.*`, outside the chain), and the rows above.
- **Status (Phase 3 batch 2, Phase 2).** Partially done in `Oversampler.*` and `Saturator.*` ([03 §6.4](03-dsp-design.md#64-oversampling-and-aliasing), §0.6). (1) Harder drive: first-order ADAA on all three curves (closed-form antiderivatives in double; the ADAA half-sample delay is taken back by the stage-1 decimator, whose taps are centred a fraction of a sample early, so the latency and the alignment with the dry path are unchanged) at every rate and profile, and 8× in Quality below 88.2 kHz (a third, 9-tap half-band); from 176.4 kHz 2× through a decimator. Saturator alone, worst of the 1 / 5 / 7 / 10 kHz rows, before → after, 44.1 kHz Quality: Tape 24 dB −28.2 → −73.0, Tube 24 dB −38.0 → −87.6, Digital 24 dB −35.6 → −83.9, 0 dBFS at 9 dB −45.9 → −92.5 dBc; Balanced / Low Latency: −28.0 → −54.1, −37.9 → −64.3, −35.5 → −62.6, −45.8 → −73.2 dBc; 48 kHz Balanced Tape 24 dB −25.1 → −59.3 dBc; 96 and 192 kHz, every profile: Tape 24 dB −45.6 … −46.1 → −75.7, Digital −59.4 … −59.8 → −87.3 … −90.9 dBc; Warmth 100's Tape 9 dB row −78.5 → −106.0 (Quality), −74.2 → −75.3 dBc (Balanced). Through the chain (`quality --set sat.on=1 --set sat.drive=24 --rate 44100`): Quality −28.2 → −73.0, Balanced −27.9 → −54.1 dBc. The ≤ −70 / −60 dBc rows are met in Quality at every rate and setting, and in Balanced / Low Latency from 88.2 kHz and for Warmth 100 and 0 dBFS at every rate (the old KnownGap row *0 dBFS at 9 dB* is closed); open: 24 dB of drive in Balanced / Low Latency at 44.1 / 48 kHz (Tape −54.1 / −59.3, Tube −64.3, Digital −62.6 dBc at 44.1 kHz), re-pinned in the KnownGap ratchet: it needs 8× there as well. The THD+N telemetry reads the same with ADAA (within 0.1 dB, Tape and Tube at 0.1–12 dB), so the governor's calibration holds; ADAA's own error is −0.04 dB on a 5 kHz fundamental at 12 dB (4×). CPU: every oversampler FIR now sums in four interleaved partial sums, which vectorises; realtime factor with the saturator at 9 dB (CLI, 20 s of pink noise, 48 kHz): Balanced 20.4 → 21.2× (+4 %), Quality 18.2 → 16.0× (−12 %; Warmth 100, which now drives Tube at up to 0.9 dB, −17 %), so against the pre-E10 build Quality is about 31 % slower with the saturator on (over the ≤ 25 % row: the price of 8×) and Balanced about 11 %. (2) Ultrasonic band-limiting at 88.2 kHz and above: not done. A steep linear-phase LP22k on the deviation needs about 100 samples at 192 kHz and a minimum-phase one would turn the deviation against the dry path. The Done-when's row (every macro at 100, 192 kHz) reads −61.7 / −62.3 dB (Balanced / Quality) on this tree, met because [E14](#e14)'s Warmth no longer drives tape saturation; with `warmth.tapeGrit` −54.3 dB, and 24 dB of drive alone −47.3 dB. (3) The MixEngine master limiter has had the LF-safe envelope since [E05](#e05) step 6; the E10 rows for it are now tested: 0.35 sin 100 Hz + 0.35 cos 200 Hz on two strips, 3.9 dB over the master's ceiling: −178.7 dBFS DC (the same limiter on the plain envelope −46.2 dBFS), and the 11-rate true-peak matrix for the master (sample peak under −1 dBTP, true peak within 0.1 dB, no safety clamp). Tests: `tests/test_saturator.cpp` (4 *Saturator ADAA* cases), `tests/test_signal_hygiene.cpp` (the table, three extreme-setting cases, the re-pinned ratchet), `tests/test_limiter.cpp` (2 master cases). Render diff: no audio value of any factory preset moves beyond 0.01 dB; `thdnMaxDb` of Warm Vinyl (−0.13 dB) and Lo-Fi Chill (−0.04 dB) does. Open: the Balanced / Low Latency 24 dB rows, LP22k, the maximizer clipper's oversampling in the table (its defaults read −97 dBc; with the crest gate off and a 24 dB cap, 24 dB of drive at 44.1 kHz −42 dBc in Quality / Balanced, −19 dBc in Low Latency) and an ADAA clipper. *Review (Phase 3 batch 2):* render baseline re-recorded (Warm Vinyl `thdnMaxDb` only); the quality ratchet's nightly all-macros rows re-recorded at today's values (alias −34.4 → −49.99 dBc, ultrasonic −55.46 → −58.81 dB; gcc, clang).

- **Status (Phase 3 batch 2, clipper table and 8× evaluation).** Partially done in `Oversampler.*`, `LoudnessMaximizer.*` and `ProcessingChain.*` ([03 §11.6](03-dsp-design.md#116-latency--cpu), [§6.4](03-dsp-design.md#64-oversampling-and-aliasing)). (1) The maximizer clipper's oversampling is in a rate-aware table, `Oversampler::forClipper (profile, rate)`, at each profile's old latency: Low Latency below 88.2 kHz runs the saturator's 16-sample 4× design (without ADAA) instead of 2× Low; Quality / Balanced keep 4× High, and 2× Low stays from 88.2 kHz. Worst in-band alias of −6 dBFS 1 / 5 / 7 / 10 kHz sines, maximizer alone with the crest gate off and the depth uncapped, Low Latency before → after: 24 dB of drive −19.0 → −42.0 dBc (44.1 kHz), −22.5 → −44.0 dBc (48 kHz); 12 dB −34.0 → −57.3 / −47.1 → −64.6 dBc; at the defaults a steady tone stays unclipped (−96.8 dBc). Cost: the maximizer in Low Latency 261 → 353 ns per stereo sample at 44.1 kHz; the CLI's realtime factor on pink noise at 44.1 kHz, Low Latency defaults, 40.8 → 34.9× (−14 %; with the saturator at 9 dB 24.9 → 21.8×). 8× in Quality / Balanced's 36 samples was measured (24 / 12 dB −42.3 → −52.7 / −57.4 → −82.8 dBc at 44.1 kHz) and not adopted: 60 % more of the maximizer's CPU for settings off the defaults. (2) 8× for the saturator in Balanced / Low Latency, evaluated: it fits their 16 samples (4 + 8 + 3 + 1) and meets every 24 dB row (44.1 kHz: Tape −54.1 → −73.1, Tube −64.3 → −74.7, Digital −62.6 → −73.4 dBc; 48 kHz: Tape −59.3 → −73.1 dBc), but doubles the curve's cost (saturator alone 15.4 → 29.1 ms per second of stereo audio at 44.1 kHz); the CLI's realtime factor with the saturator at 9 dB falls 22.7 → 17.0× (Balanced) and 22.4 → 14.7× (Low Latency), past the ≤ 25 % row, so it is not in the table and the rows stay pinned (*Signal hygiene KnownGap: 24 dB of drive ...*). Tests: `tests/test_maximizer.cpp` (the clipper table and its Low Latency rows; *LoudnessMaximizer KnownGap: the clipper at extreme settings ...* pins the 24 dB rows at −42.3 / −44.1 dBc, Low Latency −42.0 / −44.0 dBc), `tests/test_saturator.cpp` (the 8× candidate). Render diff: no factory preset value moves (0.01 dB on all presets; 0.0001 dB on the three Low Latency presets and Warm Vinyl / Bass Head). Other movement, all from the Low Latency clipper: *DC at max.drive 24, Low Latency* −142.16 → −141.92 dBFS, the ratchet's `E10.all100.dc` (Low Latency) −85.19 → −85.08 dBFS, Competitive FPS's shot lift in the 3.2 kHz band 0.26 → 0.25 dB (all pass). Open: the 24 dB rows for the clipper in every profile (an ADAA clipper: the depth cap's blend has no closed-form antiderivative) and for the saturator in Balanced / Low Latency at 44.1 / 48 kHz (8×, when the CPU budget allows) and LP22k (infeasible, above). *Review (Phase 3 batch 2):* the moves re-measured against `fe67dac` (only the three named values; the render diff unchanged at 0.0 dB tolerance, so no golden file or baseline was re-recorded for it); [01 §5.1](01-architecture.md#51-algorithmic-latency-per-profile-48-khz-the-only-latency-sources-in-the-chain)'s clipper row, the chain diagram and the overload note, [08](08-pitfalls-and-solutions.md)'s aliasing note and [03 §14.6](03-dsp-design.md#146-bypass-ab-and-latency-profiles)'s latency table (whose saturator column still read 2×) updated.
- **What.** A residual-path DC high-pass after every nonlinear stage; per-sample input sanitising (non-finite and finite values above +24 dBFS) at the strip input and in each capture FIFO, with contaminated blocks hidden from control loops. Oversampling chosen by profile and rate with ≥ 100 dB half-band stopbands, or first-order ADAA in Low Latency. Band-limit harmonic residuals at 20–22 kHz at 88.2 kHz and above. A chain alias/DC/ultrasonic test matrix.
- **Why.** Music: Warmth and Boost add inharmonic aliases and steady DC on the headphone driver. Gaming: one corrupt sample from a game or driver causes seconds of disturbance, a hearing-safety issue.
- **Expected benefit.** DC: an asymmetric 100 + 200 Hz signal gives −20.9 dBFS output DC at max.drive 12 and −12.3 dBFS at 24 (input −204 dBFS). A single 1e30 sample: about 100 ms of silence, then +6.6 dB for seconds 3–4 and −2.1 dB for 5–6, lasting at least 4 s.
