Aliases: Warmth 100 at 44.1 kHz −44.6 dBc, sat.drive 24 −16.8 dBc (the saturator runs 2x Low in Balanced and Low Latency); FabFilter/Ozone-class tools stay below −90 dBc. At 192 kHz, 20–96 kHz holds −22.2 dB of total energy with all macros. Correction: NaN handling is better than claimed (240 ms of disturbance, no lasting change). That figure was measured on the round-7 build; the explanation belongs to `d096a4d`, which introduced `resetSignalState()` so that a dropped block no longer resets AutoLevel, AutoDrive, the governor or LoudnessMatch. The NaN burst has not been re-measured on a `d096a4d` build, so the 240 ms figure is a pre-`d096a4d` value. The 1e30 spike *was* re-measured on a `d096a4d` build, with the same result as the round-7 build (100 ms of silence, then +13.0 dB at 3.1–3.5 s, +3.5 dB at 4–5 s, −2.1 dB at 5–6 s on −20 dBFS pink with Signature): finite garbage bypasses the non-finite guard, which is why step (2) below is needed.
- **Approach.** (1) y = x + HP₅Hz(clip(x) − x), double state, in the maximizer clipper, Saturator, bass harmonics and air exciter (matching the delta-oversampling design of `Oversampler.h`). (2) Keep the non-finite block drop; additionally clamp and count |x| > 15.85 and set a per-block "contaminated" flag that AutoLevel, AutoDrive, governor detectors and LoudnessMatch skip. Sanitise in `DriftCompensatedFifo::push`. On Linux apps are mixed inside PipeWire before Flubsound Pro sees them, so cross-app NaN cannot be prevented there; document it. (3) Table keyed on (profile, rate): Quality/Balanced 4x with a redesigned ≥ 100 dB stage at 44.1/48 kHz, 2x at 88.2/96, 1x + residual LP22k at ≥ 176.4; Low Latency 2x + ADAA on Tape/Tube/Digital. Only go to 8x if 4x misses −70 dBc (`Oversampler::prepare` accepts only 1, 2, 4 today). (4) New `tests/test_signal_hygiene.cpp`.
- **Depends on.** None for Phase 1. Phase 2 stays inside the [01 §5][01-5] budget, re-checks the governor's THD+N calibration, and relies on the in-flight engine swap for live profile changes.
- **Done when.** DC at max.drive 24 ≤ −60 dBFS in every profile. The 1e30 spike disturbs ≤ 50 ms above −60 dBFS and changes level by ≤ 0.3 dB after 100 ms. The NaN burst does not regress. Worst in-band inharmonic ≤ −70 dBc (Quality/Balanced) and ≤ −60 dBc (Low Latency) for 1/5/7/10 kHz sines at extreme settings at 44.1/48/96/192 kHz. At 192 kHz, 22–96 kHz energy ≤ −60 dB. Realtime factor drops ≤ 25 %; [01 §5.1][01-51] table updated from measured values.
- **Evidence.** [ProcessingChain.cpp][PC] `prepare()` (`saturator.setOversampling(2, Low)`), non-finite guard in `process()`; [test_saturator.cpp][T-sat] tests only 4x HQ; [Saturator.cpp][SAT] (only DC blocker is the Tube HP); [Oversampler.cpp][OS] `prepare`, Kaiser β 4.5; [08 B3][08-B3], [08 B5][08-B5].

<a id="e12"></a>
#### E12 · Headphone stereo imaging for music: virtual speakers, a proper crossfeed, and a centre-safe imager

*Music · benefit 4 · difficulty High · Phase A 3–4 weeks; total 12–18 engineer-weeks · Ambitious*

- **What.** Phase A: an energy-preserving L/R crossfeed with correct ITD (0.25–0.3 ms), head shadow and near-ear compensation, with bs2b and Meier presets (the current M/S shelf becomes "Mono-safe"); "Space" rebuilt as a band-weighted decorrelator with bounded ILD; width above 1 made frequency-dependent so it never writes strong anti-phase into the far ear. Phase B: "Virtual Speakers" for stereo strips using the virtualiser's existing Stereo layout (±30°), with an FDN room, diffuse-field EQ, phantom-centre handling, loudness match and headphone gating. Phase C: a partitioned convolver with BRIR/SOFA loading.
- **Why.** Nearly all music is stereo and stays inside the head. Crossfeed narrows the bass rather than externalising, Space smears centred vocals, and width > 1 collapses hard-panned ILD. Externalisation is the main selling point of Dolby Atmos for Headphones, Waves Nx, Boom 3D and SoundID Virtual Monitoring.
- **Expected benefit.** (A) Certain and cheap: removes three defects that harm stereo music today (crossfeed interaural delay 0.24 *samples*; up to 9.3 dB ILD on a centred impulse with space = 1; width 2 puts the far ear at −6.0 dB anti-phase). (B) Uncertain: the only renderer is a Brown-Duda sphere with first-order shadow, a rear shelf and 6 early taps, and no pinna cues. Frontal externalisation will be weak and front/back confusion likely. Waves Nx, SoundID and Dolby compete on data this model lacks, so Phase B is judged by a panel before it ships default-on.
- **Approach.** Phase A: far-ear head-shadow LP (bs2b 700 Hz / 4.5 dB, Meier 650 Hz / 9.5 dB), fractional delay with the virtualiser's 3rd-order Lagrange, near-ear compensation for a flat L+R power sum; complementary velvet-noise decorrelators off below 300 Hz and −6 dB at 1–4 kHz with an ILD limiter on the correlated part; width > 1 only above `spatial.lowCut` with an S/M cap plus decorrelation (the STFT pan-index remap only in Quality, or dropped). Phase B: a `virt.stereo` key makes `binaural` true on stereo strips (no structural prepare needed); 16-line Hadamard FDN; diffuse-field EQ from the sphere model; EQ on M with the inverse of the averaged ±30° comb; the M2 Width macro drives room width and DRR on headphone output. Phase C: shared partitioned convolver ([E29](#e29)).
- **Depends on.** Phase A: nothing. Phase B: a manual "output is headphones" setting (DeviceProfiles is family-level, [E16](#e16)) and the listening panel. Phase C: the convolver. Re-run preset intent checks ([E14](#e14)) after crossfeed/Space change.
- **Done when.** Crossfeed ITD 0.22–0.30 ms by cross-correlation; L+R power flat within 0.5 dB; Space on a centred impulse ≤ 2 dB ILD per 1/3 octave; width 2: far-ear anti-phase ≤ −20 dB below lowCut, mono-sum loss ≤ 3 dB; no toggle click. Phase B: centred-vocal spectrum flat within ±1.5 dB (100 Hz–10 kHz), loudness match ≤ 0.3 LU. Go/no-go: blind MUSHRA (≥ 12 listeners, 6 tracks) against plain stereo and one commercial reference.
- **Evidence.** [ProcessingChain.cpp][PC] `binaural = config.inputChannels > 2 && ...`; [test_virtualizer.cpp][T-virt] "stereo layout renders two mirrored virtual speakers" (unreachable in the product); [StereoSpatializer.cpp][SS] `kCrossfeedHz 700`, `kCrossfeedScale 0.6`, `kAllPassG 0.5`; [HeadphoneVirtualizer.h][HVh] renderer description; [08 C3][08-C3].
- **Status (Phase 2 batch 1).** Phase A partially done in `StereoSpatializer.*`; five `StereoSpatializer (E12)` cases in `tests/test_spatializer.cpp` print every figure below ([03 §7](03-dsp-design.md#7-stereo-widener--spatializer)).
  - **(1) Crossfeed.** Bs2b (700 Hz, 4.5 dB, the new default) and Meier (650 Hz, 9.5 dB) are an energy-preserving L/R crossfeed: head-shadow low-pass, far ear delayed 0.235 ms through the 3rd-order Lagrange, near-ear shelf with n0² + g² = 1. The M/S shelf became `CrossfeedType::MonoSafe`. Measured:
    - ITD by cross-correlation of hard-left white noise 0.03 ms (1.4 samples on this test; 0.24 samples in the audit) → 0.27 ms at 44.1 / 48 kHz and 0.25 ms at 96 / 192 kHz.
    - Hard-panned L+R power range 2.43 dB → ±0.07 dB; feed level at 50 Hz 4.52 / 9.52 dB.
    - Centred sources now sum coherently: +2.7 / +2.0 dB at low frequencies at crossfeed 1, +1.3 dB at 0.3. The mono sum is no longer exact with this crossfeed; the Mono-safe type keeps it exact.
  - **(2) Space.** HP 300 Hz kept, presence dip added (−7 dB at 2 kHz, Q 0.4, −5 dB at 1 / 4 kHz), pre-delay 5 → 10 ms. Measured:
    - Centred-impulse ILD per 1/3 octave 1.91 → 0.70 dB.
    - Steady 1–4 kHz tones 9.27 → 4.88 dB.
    - Steady tones at 300–700 Hz and above 5 kHz keep up to 9.2 dB (KNOWN_GAP).
    - The velvet-noise decorrelator was not adopted: the flat all-pass network was kept.
    - A zero-latency limiter on the correlated part was prototyped and not shipped. It took a pure 1.5 kHz tone 4.1 → 1.6 dB, but a 220 Hz harmonic tone stayed at 5.7 → 5.7 dB per partial.
  - **(3) Width.** A polarity guard caps the added side signal at M (peak envelopes above `spatial.lowCut`). Measured:
    - At width 2 a hard-panned source's far ear goes from −6.0 dB anti-phase (−15.3 dB at 100 Hz) to exact silence (Done-when ≤ −20 dB).
    - R = L/2 still widens 6 → 13.7 / 14.0 dB ILD (1 / 5 kHz). Mono-sum loss 0 dB (M untouched).
  - **Click-free.** Every crossfeed type switch, crossfeed toggle and width 1 ↔ 2 passes the curvature test.
  - **CPU.** 23 → 26 ns neutral; 28 → 41 ns with width 1.6, focus / space 0.5 and Bs2b at 0.5.
  - **Side effects.** Preset render diff, isolated before / after build:
    - Classical & Jazz, Earbuds, Bluetooth Headphones and Audiophile Subtle (the presets that store crossfeed): +0.7 to +1.3 dB at 31.5–250 Hz; the music programme +0.57 to +1.06 LU integrated, true peak up to +1.15 dB; −0.1 to −0.4 dB at 1 kHz on the game programmes.
    - Wide Stage and Lo-Fi Chill: true peak +0.19 / +0.17 dB. Cinematic Adventure: within tolerance.
    - Every other render bit-identical. `tests/golden/preset-render-baseline.json` re-recorded for these seven presets only.
    - [E10](#e10)'s NaN-burst disturbance 799.8 → 441.0 ms: Signature's width 1.23 no longer widens uncorrelated pink noise (799.8 ms with the guard disabled).
  - **Open.**
    - A `spatial.crossfeedType` key with UI and preset field (the chain always runs Bs2b).
    - The re-voicing of those presets ([E14](#e14)).
    - Width's "plus decorrelation".
    - A band- or bin-resolved guard: the guard reads the band mix, so a hard-left pink 6 dB under a centred one keeps a −8.3 dB anti-phase far-ear copy, as before.
    - Per-partial Space ILD (STFT, Quality).
    - Phases B and C.
- **Status (p3b3, Phase A remainder: `spatial.crossfeedType`).** The open key is done. `spatial.crossfeedType` is a Choice (Bs2b / Meier / Mono-safe, "Stereo" group), appended to `Parameters.*` at layout version 6. `ProcessingChain` passes it to `SpatializerParams::crossfeedType`, and a change cross-fades in the spatializer: the old model glides out, the new one in, and the head-shadow corner glides (20 ms). Bs2b is the default, the model the chain always ran before. A preset or state without the key therefore loads Bs2b and renders bit-identically, and a preset stores the type by label. It is a preset field, a plug-in host parameter (version hint 6), a CLI `--set spatial.crossfeedType=Meier` / `mono-safe` / index key (in `params`, and a wrong label is an error), a generic control in the Stereo card's expanded grid, and it has its ParamHints hint. Measured through the chain (only Stereo & Space on, crossfeed 1, 48 kHz; `tests/test_spatializer.cpp`, three new cases):
  - **ITD by cross-correlation** (before → after): 0.27 ms Bs2b only → 0.268 ms Bs2b, 0.268 ms Meier, 0.028 ms Mono-safe (Done-when 0.22–0.30 met for Bs2b and Meier).
  - **Feed at 50 Hz:** 4.53 / 9.53 dB (Mono-safe 7.40 dB).
  - **Hard-panned L+R, octaves 31.5 Hz–16 kHz:** −0.04 … +0.07 dB for Bs2b and Meier (within 0.5 dB met).
  - **Mono-safe mono sum:** within 4.5e−8 of the crossfeed-0 render (exact up to float rounding), where Bs2b / Meier do not preserve it.
  - **Type changes:** every pair switched through the store while playing a panned 300 Hz sine peaks at 1.01× the steady renders' curvature (limit 2×: no click).
  - **With crossfeed 0, or in Gaming:** the three types render bit-identically.
  - **Default renders identical:** 125 / 125 outputs (25 factory presets × 5 programmes) bit-identical, by SHA-256 of every WAV against the pre-change build of the same tree, and the render diff at 0 dB tolerance finds no change.
  - **Moves, re-recorded once by the batch 3 review:** `tests/golden/parameter-defaults.json` gains one key (`spatial.crossfeedType` 0), and all 25 `contentHash` values in `tests/golden/factory-presets.json` change, because the new key is in the resolved values. No golden render, no `preset-render-baseline.json` entry, no KnownGap and no quality-ratchet value moves (the default is the previous behaviour, bit for bit; the review's render diff finds no move outside the E21 presets).
  - **Open:** a named key on the Advanced view's Stereo card face (`ModuleCard.cpp`, the app-surfaces unit; the expanded grid shows it now). Re-voicing the four crossfeed presets onto Meier or Mono-safe is gated on the listening panel ([E14](#e14)). The Quality-only STFT residue (per-partial Space ILD, band-resolved width guard, width decorrelation) and Phases B and C are descoped or gated ([§5.4](#54-phase-3--following-two-quarters-weeks-1540-about-months-49)).
- **Status (p3b5, app card).** `spatial.crossfeedType` is on the Stereo & Space card of the module rack (a choice, *Crossfeed Type*), not only in the expanded card's grid: the p3b3 line's open item is closed (`ui/ModuleCard.cpp` table; `tests/app/test_app_ui_guards.cpp`, screenshot state `module-keys`). No sound moves.

<a id="e14"></a>
#### E14 · Music voicing: a real Warmth macro, differentiated genre and device-class presets, and verified preset intent

*Presets · benefit 4 · difficulty Medium · 4–7 engineer-weeks*

- **Status (Phase 3 batch 2, owner feedback: Warmth remap).** Approach step (1) done, partially: the Warmth remap only; the intent blocks (2), the genre presets (3) and the listening panel (4) remain. Owner feedback from the first real test (Windows, Turtle Beach headset): Music Warmth 0 → 100 "seems to do nothing" (measured: v1 Warmth moved pink noise by +0.4 dB at 63 Hz and −0.5 dB everywhere else). Now Warmth is a tone control ([03 §14.13](03-dsp-design.md#1413-warmth-tilt-chain)): a dedicated 2-section tilt, `ToneTilt` (`dsp/ToneTilt.*`, not E09's slots 10–15, which stay [E09](#e09)'s), ahead of the module slots (after the preamp, contour, gate and neural slot, before the parametric EQ and every stage the SafetyGovernor scales or taps): a body bell at 200 Hz, Q 0.7, +3.5 dB and a high shelf at 7 kHz, Q 0.707, −3.0 dB at 100 %, linear in dB with `warmth.tone` (the macro's smoothstep maps knob 25 / 50 / 75 % to 16 / 50 / 84 %), glided (0 → 1 in 200 ms, coefficients interpolated per sample), idle and bit-exact at 0. Automatic level compensation, open loop: the K-weighted loudness of the stage's input with and without the full tilt (3 s one-pole, a running mean after a start), trim −amount × that change. New parameters (layout version 5, both 0 / off, migration-safe: a preset that leaves Warmth at 0 is bit-identical): `warmth.tone` (the Warmth macro adds up to 1, ungoverned) and `warmth.tapeGrit` (Warmth keeps its v1 rows). MacroMap: the Music M5 rows became two sets chosen by the base `warmth.tapeGrit` (tone: tilt, Saturation on, drive +0.9 dB governed; v1: Tape drive +9 dB, bass +2 dB, harmonics +0.2, Bass on) and a new override row kind (`MacroOverride`) that selects Tube while the saturator is Warmth's alone (`sat.on` off and `sat.type` at its default in the base values; a saturator the user or preset switched on keeps its type). Lo-Fi Chill and Warm Vinyl set `warmth.tapeGrit` and keep their Tape grit bit for bit. Gaming's M5 (Voice & Score) and every Boost row are unchanged. **Verifier's changes** (an independent re-measurement with its own build, probe and numpy analysis, then fixes): (a) the first version's low shelf (300 Hz, +3.8 dB) lifted the sub-bass that carries most of the K-weighted loudness of bass-heavy music, so the level compensation (−3.2 dB) left the 250 Hz octave 0.6 dB lower than at Warmth 0 and the vocal range 3 dB lower (a synthetic mix at Warmth 100: +0.8 / −0.6 / −3.0 / −5.7 dB at 63 Hz / 250 Hz / 1 kHz / 12.5 kHz): "quieter and duller", not "warmer"; with the body bell the same mix reads −0.4 / +1.9 / −0.9 / −4.0 dB, and Flubsound Signature +0.6 / +1.6 / −1.0 / −4.3 dB at 125 Hz / 250 Hz / 1 kHz / 12.5 kHz; (b) the automatic preamp's model counted the sections but not the trim, so with `auto.preamp` on Warmth 100 read −2.8 LU on every programme; it now counts the trim at the target amount (0.25 dB steps; the drum programme −2.8 → −1.6 LU, the preamp's own headroom for the bell; pink, whose trim is 0, −2.8 LU); (c) placement between the Clarity and Saturation slots → ahead of the EQ slot: there the bell also lifted the bass engine's generated harmonics and the governed stages ahead of it moved the measure (with the bell in that place the Strict all-macros 50 Hz scene read −21.3 dB); (d) docs: the knob's smoothstep, the new numbers. Done-when rows (`tests/test_warmth.cpp`, −6 dBFS 1 kHz, Warmth 50 / 100; before: Tape +9 dB, Warmth 50 THD+N 2.99 %, H2 −146 / H3 −30.5 dB): THD+N 0.08 / 0.30 % at 44.1 / 48 / 96 kHz, maximizer off and on (≤ 0.5 % met), H2 −63.0 / −51.1 dBc over H3 −70.7 / −58.3 dBc (H2 > H3 met), worst inharmonic −117.8 / −111.9 dBc at 44.1 kHz and −114.4 / −108.5 dBc at 48 kHz (≤ −80 met); pink transfer re Warmth 0 at 200 Hz / 1 kHz / 10 kHz +1.75 / +0.17 / −1.25 dB and **+3.50 / +0.34 / −2.49 dB** (48 kHz; 44.1 kHz within 0.05 dB; all three latency profiles identical); integrated loudness re Warmth 0 −0.11 / −0.02 LU on pink, −0.04 / −0.05 LU on the render diff's drum-and-bass programme, −0.05 / +0.01 LU on the synthetic mix, at 44.1, 48 and 96 kHz in every profile (≤ 0.3 LU met; trim on the drum programme −0.37 / −0.73 dB, over 2–6 s of kicks it moves 0.04 dB). Robustness: a Warmth step 0 → 100 or 100 → 0 in one 64- or 512-sample block keeps the largest second difference at the held settings' and everything above 3 kHz under −100 dBFS; held settings render bit-identically at blocks of 64, 512, 4096 and ragged sizes; no non-finite sample with `sat.drive` 24, `bass.boost` 15, Boost 100 + Loudness 100 or every macro at 100, at protection Off / Normal / Strict, and with the maximizer on the true peak stays ≤ −1.00 dBTP wherever Warmth 0 met the ceiling (`sat.drive` 24 at Strict −3.3 → −2.6 dBTP on pink; `bass.boost` 15 −1.28 → −1.00 dBTP), while at Boost + Loudness 100, where the limiter already overshoots at Warmth 0 (−0.81 dBTP on the drum programme), it reads −0.74 dBTP; 7.1 input −0.01 LU; every Gaming preset (also with Voice & Score and Boost at 100, at Normal and Strict) and every Music preset as stored bit-exact against the pre-change build; the tests deterministic over 50 runs, each case < 0.75 s. **Deviation from this item's ±1.5 dB (±0.3):** about twice that at 100 % (+3.5 / −2.5 dB on pink) on purpose, since the owner heard nothing; half at Warmth 50; and the low section is a body bell, not a shelf (a). The drive range shrank 9 → 0.9 dB, not by 10–15 dB (that would be below 0 dB; the depth blend makes 0.9 dB the calibrated equivalent). Step (d): the v1 bass boost and harmonics are not in the tone set. Moves for the batch 2 reviewer (before → after; nothing re-recorded here): `tests/golden/parameter-defaults.json` two new keys (`warmth.tone` 0, `warmth.tapeGrit` 0); `tests/golden/factory-presets.json` all 25 `contentHash` values (new parameters in the hash; Lo-Fi Chill and Warm Vinyl also for their new key); golden renders unchanged; `tests/golden/preset-render-baseline.json` no move (125 renders within 0.1 dB of a baseline recorded with the pre-change build of the same tree; against the committed baseline the same 119 pre-existing values move with and without this change). KnownGap *all Music macros at 100 on a 50 Hz sine* (E06; v1 → now): Off THD+N −13.96 → −16.99 dB (pin −13.85), driven −12.96 → −17.21 dB (pin −12.96), harmonics share −13.58 → −16.11 dB (pin −13.58): better, the pins need re-basing; Strict −35.79 → −34.85 dB (met) and driven −27.22 → −23.06 dB (≤ −22.96: met by 0.10 dB); **Normal −36.28 → −25.99 dB, past its ≤ −30.46 dB row** (driven −32.57 → −38.72, met). That row reads the SafetyGovernor's slow recovery: the scale sits at its 0.30 floor from 2 s and ramps up again, and a ramp inside the 6 – 10 s window counts as THD+N in the steady-sine fit. The tilt is transparent on a steady 50 Hz sine (bell +0.45 dB, trim −0.45 dB), so the scene now behaves as it does without Warmth: macros 1–4 at 100 with Warmth 0 read −27.7 dB on the same build, and the first version (low shelf) read −26.0 dB at Warmth 50; the v1 Warmth's +9 dB Tape and the first version's shelf at Warmth ≥ 80 moved the ramp before 6 s. A decision for E06's owner (the row's window or a gain-tracking fit), not a Warmth defect. Quality ratchet (`tests/quality_targets.json`, same build pair): warmth-100 alias at 44.1 kHz −75.8 → −112.8 dBc (Balanced), DC −80.6 → −150.8 dBFS, ultrasonic −69.9 → −114.5 dB; music-all-100 alias −35.1 → −50.0 dBc (recorded −34.4, still a gap), ultrasonic −54.4 → −58.8 dB (recorded −55.46; the pre-change build of this tree read it as REGRESSED, now within, not yet ≤ −60), DC −77.9 → −86.8 dBFS; the `warmth-100` setting's "about" text (sat.drive 9 Tape, bass) is stale. Tests outside this change's files that encode the v1 Warmth: `test_distortion.cpp` *base saturation alone trips the governor ... only the governed Warmth contributions are scaled* fails (it expects +9 dB × scale; one line, `store.set (WarmthTapeGrit, 1.0f)`, restores the v1 scenario, and `test_warmth.cpp` checks that scenario); the docs/03 §14.8 line describing it. Not this change: the Scenes gaming matrix (fails identically before) and the saturator latency rows (the concurrent Saturator / Oversampler work). Open: the app's indication that Warmth chose Tube and a `warmth.tapeGrit` control beyond the generic parameter list; a user preset that raised Warmth gets the new Warmth unless it sets `warmth.tapeGrit` (a macro-map change, T5: release note); with `auto.preamp` on, Warmth costs level where the trim is small; steps (2)–(4). *Review (Phase 3 batch 2):* re-recorded once: `parameter-defaults.json` (+`warmth.tone` 0, `warmth.tapeGrit` 0, with E02's and E11's three keys) and every `contentHash` in `factory-presets.json` (golden renders of the Warmth-free presets and of Lo-Fi Chill / Warm Vinyl unmoved by this change); the E06 decision taken: its Normal / Strict rows now read THD+N with the level drift tracked and pass (E06's review line), Off re-pinned to the numbers above; `tests/quality_targets.json`'s `warmth-100` description updated.
- **Status (p3b3, app).** The app remainders are closed (`ui/ModuleCard.*`, `ui/ModuleRack.*`, `ui/BoostPanel.*`): in Music a chip beside the Warmth macro's value in the Boost panel says what Warmth does to the saturator now, *TUBE* while its override row chose the Tube saturator, *TAPE* with `warmth.tapeGrit` on, else *TONE* (the tilt alone, or the user's own saturator, which keeps its type), with a tooltip saying why; a click on it switches Tape Grit. The Saturation card has a **Tape Grit** switch (it acts with the saturator off, so it is not dimmed with it) and the note *Tube chosen by Warmth*. Before: `warmth.tapeGrit` only in the generic grid, and nothing showed the Tube choice. Tested in `tests/app/test_app_ui_guards.cpp` (Warmth 60 % → TUBE and the card's note; the chip writes the key, the card's switch clears it; a user saturator → TONE; Gaming → no chip); screenshot state `module-readings`. No sound, golden or parameter moves. Open as before: the release note for user presets that raised Warmth, steps (2)–(4).
- **Status (p3b4, intent blocks).** Approach step (2) done: every factory preset states its intent and `test_factory_presets` asserts every block, **25 / 25** (before: completeness, keys, ceiling and "does not mute" only). `PresetIO.*` reads an optional top-level `intent` object (`preset::Intent`: per programme the tone in ten octave bands 31.5 Hz – 16 kHz with a tolerance, the loudness offset with a tolerance, an LRA-loss bound, a THD+N maximum and, for Gaming, the step / bed contrast with a tolerance), validates it (an invalid block is dropped with a warning and the preset still loads; unknown fields are skipped with a warning) and writes it back unchanged; the engine never reads it and `contentHash` leaves it out (every factory `contentHash` and golden render unchanged; the schema stays 3.0, factory files version 1). The metrics, on programmes built sample for sample like `preset-render-diff.py`'s (checked bit-exact against it), rendered by the CLI's offline renderer in each preset's suggested profile: tone = each band's change re the input minus the integrated-loudness change, on the `quality` pink (−18 dBFS RMS, 1 – 4 s of 4 s) and on the render diff's music (its first 6 s, −21.4 LUFS); loudness offset on both; LRA loss and THD+N (`render.stats` `distortion.thdnMaxDb`) on that music continued for 6 s at −8 / −4 dB (12 s, LRA 6.7 LU); the step / bed contrast change in the 3.2 kHz band on the render diff's *game-quiet* (the [E19](#e19) contrast of `test_known_gaps.cpp`). The blocks were measured on this tree (Phase 3 batch 3's Late Night / Podcast & Voice retune in; the render diff 125 / 125 within 0.1 dB of the committed baseline) with the same tolerances for every preset: tone ±1 dB on pink and ±1.5 dB on music, loudness ±1 LU, LRA loss measured + 0.5 LU, THD+N measured + 3 dB (−100 dB where nothing distorts: 23 presets; Lo-Fi Chill −34.2 dB, Warm Vinyl −41.2 dB), contrast ±1 dB. What the blocks now say, for example: Laptop Speakers −15.5 / −8.9 dB at 31.5 / 63 Hz on pink (small-speaker mode), Podcast & Voice +8.1 – 8.4 dB over 4 – 16 kHz on music re its +4.8 LU, Bass Head +4.3 dB at 31.5 Hz, Lo-Fi Chill −6.5 dB at 16 kHz, Club Loud +6.2 / +7.4 LU, the levellers' LRA losses (Late Night 5.40, Podcast & Voice 5.46, Voice Chat 4.61, Night Mode Gaming 4.70 LU; every other preset ≤ 1.49 LU), step / bed contrast +5.4 dB (Competitive FPS), +5.0 (Battle Royale), 0.0 (Tournament Clean, which reads 0.0 everywhere). One case per preset (*Factory presets: intent - <category> - <name>*, registered from the folder, so a new preset is checked without a new case; at most 1.4 s each, Quality presets included) and one completeness case; `FLUB_INTENT_PRINT=1` prints each measured block in the file's format, the documented procedure for a deliberate re-voicing ([presets/README.md](../presets/README.md) *Intent blocks*); `tests/test_presets_schema.cpp` covers parsing, validation messages, the round trip and the hash. **The Classical & Jazz row is not met, pinned:** on a −10.5 LUFS pop master (the render diff's music in 4 s sections at 0 / −6 / 0 / −4 dB, through a −0.3 dBTP true-peak limiter: −10.51 LUFS, −0.35 dBTP, LRA 4.56 LU) Classical & Jazz Dynamic with `auto.preamp` on loses **1.42 LU** (target ≤ 0.3; the limiter deeper than 1 dB 59 % of the time), 1.48 LU without the preamp, 0.97 LU with `auto.preampHot` too. Cause: the master's peaks are 0.65 dB over the preset's −1 dBTP ceiling and the preset lifts such programme by about 1.6 LU (crossfeed about 1.0, the 1.2 dB bass shelf about 0.5 LU), so the limiter holds the loud sections down and not the quiet ones; `auto.preamp` takes off only static boosts past its 1 dB allowance, and the hot term reacts only once the loud section's peaks arrive. Closing it needs [E11](#e11)'s open intro / loud-section work (a preamp that knows the track's peak ahead) or a re-voicing of the preset (an owner decision); *KnownGap: Classical & Jazz Dynamic …* (three cases in `test_factory_presets.cpp`). No render, golden, render-baseline or `contentHash` entry moves; nothing to re-record. Open: steps (3) (the genre presets, next) and (4) (the panel); the Classical & Jazz row above.
- **Status (p3b4, genre presets).** Approach step (3) done: five new Music presets, the library 25 → **30** factory presets (18 Music, 9 Gaming, 3 Device): *Rock & Metal*, *Orchestral & Film*, *Acoustic & Singer-Songwriter*, *R&B & Vocal* and *Electronic & Ambient* (`presets/factory/music-rock-metal.json`, `-orchestral-film`, `-acoustic-singer-songwriter`, `-rnb-vocal`, `-electronic-ambient`), each with a description, six tags for the browser (the two crossfeed presets tagged `headphones`, still the most common tag, 4 → 6; device suggestions stay [E40](#e40)'s: the device profiles suggest Flubsound Signature, and none of the five is a Bluetooth preset), and an intent block measured on this tree with step (2)'s default tolerances; all pass their intent cases (registered from the folder, 1.2–1.3 s each) and the factory policy checks (keys and stored non-defaults, ceiling −1 dBTP and maximizer on, no fixed drive, the subsonic rule, the hot-programme and all-macros stress renders in Balanced, Low Latency and the suggested profile: sample peak under the ceiling, true peak within 0.15 dB, no safety clamp). A new case, *Factory presets: the genre presets keep the voicing their descriptions promise*, checks the effective values: Orchestral & Film without maximizer drive, clipper, glue, compressor or dynamic EQ, with Meier crossfeed and an LRA-loss bound ≤ 1 LU; Acoustic and R&B on Warmth's tone tilt (not the Tape grit, Tube chosen); none of the five with maximizer drive at its stored Boost (R&B's Boost was lowered 35 → 30 % for it, its bass shelf raised 2.5 → 3 dB to keep the low end). Voicing, conservative since no listener has heard them (pink tone re the loudness change, the loudness offset pink / music, the LRA loss on the 12 s music, THD+N): **Rock & Metal** 250 / 500 Hz −1.0 / −0.7 dB, 1 / 2 kHz +0.5 / +1.0 dB (a 1.8 kHz bell), 4 / 8 kHz −0.4 / −0.5 dB (a dynamic 5 kHz band), −0.2 / −0.3 LU, 0.31 LU, nothing distorts; **Orchestral & Film** 31.5 / 63 Hz +1.7 / +1.4 dB, 2 / 4 kHz −0.8 / −0.8 dB, −0.3 / +0.8 LU, 0.15 LU (bound 0.7), nothing distorts, slow limiter only, Quality; **Acoustic & Singer-Songwriter** 125 / 250 Hz +0.8 / +0.8 dB (Warmth 35 %), 8 / 16 kHz −0.5 / −0.1 dB (a 10 kHz shelf against Warmth's softer top), +0.5 / +0.6 LU, 0.07 LU, THD+N −76.9 dB (Warmth's Tube); **R&B & Vocal** 31.5 / 63 Hz +1.4 / +1.1 dB, 2 kHz +0.6 dB, 8 / 16 kHz −0.8 / −0.6 dB, +0.4 / +1.2 LU, 0.15 LU, −80.3 dB; **Electronic & Ambient** 31.5 / 63 Hz +2.3 / +1.4 dB, 250 Hz – 16 kHz within 0.3 dB, width 1.15 and space 0.2, +0.5 / +1.5 LU, 0.21 LU, nothing distorts. Each description states these numbers in words. Distinctness: the largest band difference to the nearest existing preset is 0.6–1.0 dB on pink (Orchestral & Film against Classical & Jazz Dynamic 0.6, Rock & Metal against Crystal Clarity 0.9, Acoustic against Wide Stage 1.0), so the five differ as much in dynamics, space and macros as in tone; whether to voice them further is the panel's (step 4). The render diff's programmes, all clean: true peak ≤ −1.49 dBTP, no clipper energy, limiter deeper than 1 dB 0 % everywhere, latency 192 samples (Orchestral & Film 1352 in Quality). Reported, not a row: on step (2)'s −10.5 LUFS pop master Orchestral & Film loses 1.28 / 1.05 LU of loudness range without / with `auto.preamp` (Classical & Jazz 1.48 / 1.42, the same cause: a bass lift on a master whose peaks sit over the ceiling). Orchestral & Film uses the Meier crossfeed as a new preset; the four existing crossfeed presets stay on Bs2b ([E12](#e12)'s re-voicing stays gated). Re-recorded once in the p3b4 review, as listed here: `tests/golden/factory-presets.json` **five new entries** (the uuids in the files; golden renders −17.10 / −16.89 / −16.60 / −16.45 / −16.50 LUFS for Rock, Orchestral, Acoustic, R&B, Electronic; until then *Golden: factory presets keep their uuid and contentHash* reports them as not recorded and the count 30 against 25), `tests/golden/preset-render-baseline.json` **25 new renders** (the five on five programmes); no existing golden render or render-baseline value moves (the render diff 125 / 125 within 0.1 dB, every recorded golden render within 0.02 dB), no parameter, KnownGap or quality-ratchet value. Tests: 25 → 30 in `tests/app/test_app_ui_preset_browser.cpp` and `test_app_first_run.cpp`. The two whole-library render cases grow with the library (11.7 → 14.2 s and 8.2 → 10.3 s here, over the 2 s guideline before too; a per-preset split like the intent cases would fix it). Open: step (4), the listening panel (perception of all 30 descriptions), and the Classical & Jazz row above.
- **Status (owner listening, 2026-10-06, Turtle Beach Stealth 600PC Gen 3, USB dongle, Windows 11; music from Microsoft Edge through per-app capture on the Music strip).** Warmth 0 ↔ 100: warmer at about the same loudness (the equal-loudness half confirmed). Width, Clarity and Loudness 0 ↔ 100: each works. Late Night: works, takes about a second to settle. Podcast & Voice: works. A/B: works. **Owner decision:** Classical & Jazz Dynamic stays as voiced (no `auto.preampAllowance` 0); its 0.72 / 0.44 LU KnownGap stays open as accepted.
- **Status (local, owner request 2026-10-06, a Synthwave preset).** A new Music preset, *Synthwave* (`presets/factory/music-synthwave.json`), the library 30 → **31** factory presets (19 Music, 9 Gaming, 3 Device), for synth-led music: synthwave / retrowave / outrun, synth-pop and progressive house / electro in the deadmau5 vein (wide sidechained pads, plucky arpeggiated leads, a four-on-the-floor kick that must stay tight). Voiced for the owner's Turtle Beach Stealth 600PC Gen 3 within his listening constraints: Punch low (5 %: it favours the kick over the snare) and Boost 30 % (≤ 45 %, below the maximizer's drive and Punch's fade). Settings: bass +5.5 dB @ 55 Hz with protection −8 dBFS, mono < 120 Hz, subsonic 20 Hz at 12 dB/oct; Warmth 30 % (the tone tilt, Tube chosen); +2.5 dB @ 3 kHz (Q 1.2) for the leads, **Relative presence** 0.2 @ 3 kHz (the first factory preset in Relative mode: it lifts less on bright masters), +1 dB high shelf @ 12 kHz and air 0.15 against Warmth's softer top; width 1.1 above 220 Hz (Width macro 30 %), space 0.15, mono safety at min. correlation 0.15; glue 0.15. Measured (MSVC, the intent machinery; pink tone re the loudness change): 31.5 / 63 / 125 Hz **+2.2 / +1.3 / 0.0 dB** (the sub lift leaves the kick's 125 Hz alone), 250 Hz – 1 kHz −0.5 … −0.8 dB, 2 / 4 kHz **+0.5 / +0.7 dB** (about 1.3 dB over the mids), 8 / 16 kHz −0.6 / −0.6 dB; music 0.6 / 0.5 / 0.0 / −0.6 / −1.5 / −1.5 / −1.0 / −0.2 / −1.3 / −1.2 dB; loudness **+1.3 / +1.9 LU** (pink / music); LRA 6.66 → 6.36 LU on the 12 s music (**0.30 LU** lost, bound 0.8); THD+N −80.1 dB (Warmth's Tube; bound −77). The render diff's programmes: true peak −1.2 dBTP on *tone-kicks* (−7.4 on *music*), limiter never deeper than 0 dB, glue −0.28 dB at most, pumping 0.01 dB, latency 192 samples. Demo pair `preset-synthwave` (matched, the pack's music): 2 / 4 / 8 / 16 kHz +1.1 / +1.9 / +0.7 / +0.4 dB, 125 Hz – 1 kHz −0.2 … −0.3 dB; its sub shelf is held back by the headroom protection on that bass-heavy programme (31.5 / 63 Hz +0.0 / +0.1 dB, as Electronic & Ambient's +0.2). Against Electronic & Ambient (the nearest by purpose): the same sub idea but with Warmth, the 3 kHz lift (4 kHz +0.7 against −0.1 dB), a 16 kHz −0.6 against 0.0 dB, less width (1.1 / Width 30 against 1.15 / 35) and less space; largest pink-band distance to any preset 0.6 dB (Bluetooth Headphones), R&B & Vocal 0.7, Electronic & Ambient 0.8. Recorded (MSVC values; the reference platform is Linux gcc, which should confirm them): `tests/golden/factory-presets.json` one new entry (golden render −16.14 LUFS), `tests/golden/preset-render-baseline.json` five new renders (confirmed on CI's gcc leg on 2026-10-08, every value equal, [E59](#e59)); no other golden render, render-baseline value (150 / 150 within 0.1 dB) or `contentHash` moves. Tests: 30 → 31 in `tests/app/test_app_ui_preset_browser.cpp`; Synthwave joins the genre-voicing case (Warmth's tilt with Tube, no maximizer drive); the demo pack 61 → 62 pairs. Not heard yet: the owner's ear on synthwave and progressive house is the check (docs/12 §12 row 55).
- **What.** Remap Warmth to a tonal tilt (about +1.5 dB low shelf at 200 Hz, −1.5 dB high shelf at 7 kHz at 100 %) plus mostly 2nd-order saturation capped at about 0.5 % THD, with automatic level compensation; keep Tape grit for Lo-Fi and Warm Vinyl. Give each preset a measurable intent block (tonal envelope, LRA-loss bound, loudness offset, max THD) asserted in tests. Add rock/metal, orchestral/film, acoustic, R&B/vocal and electronic/ambient presets. Run the planned listening-panel voicing pass.
- **Why.** Warmth at mid-knob is 3 % odd-order distortion. Presets are hard to tell apart tonally, and nothing verifies that a preset does what its description says.
- **Expected benefit.** Warmth 50 on a −6 dBFS 1 kHz sine (max off): H2 −146 dB, H3 −30.5 dB, THD+N 2.99 %. Switching to Tube alone does not fix it (Warmth 50: H2 −29.4 / H3 −33.7 dB, 3.96 %; Warmth 100: 9.84 %), so the M5 drive range (`SatDriveDb 9`) must shrink by 10–15 dB and the audible warmth must come from the tilt. The differentiation claim is weaker than first stated: presets differ in dynamics, spatial and macros, not only tone, and Classical & Jazz Dynamic keeps LRA 20.19 LU on a −17.3 LUFS classical programme (it loses 1.35 LU only on loud-pop masters). Intent tests are still needed: nothing checks LRA, THD or tone today.
- **Approach.** (1) Tilt via the spare ParametricEq slots 10–15 (`kMaxBands 16`), which conflicts with [E09](#e09), or a dedicated 2-section tilt; decide ownership first. Add a MacroMap "override" row kind for choice values, so Warmth selects Tube only when the user has not set `sat.type`, with a visible UI indication. Calibrate drive open-loop (THD ≤ 0.5 %, H2 > H3 at 100 %); do not close the loop on `DistortionEstimator`, which would make tone pump with the programme. (2) Optional `intent` object in preset JSON, asserted through the offline renderer; no pairwise "presets must differ by 1 dB" test. (3) Genre presets with intent blocks; device classes belong to [E15](#e15). (4) Panel per roadmap 1.4 (8 listeners, 40 tracks, 6 games), results in docs/10.
- **Depends on.** [E11](#e11) (LRA bounds on loud masters), the E09 slot decision, [E12](#e12) Phase A (re-voices crossfeed and Space), a panel owner.
- **Done when.** Warmth 50 and 100 at −6 dBFS 1 kHz: THD+N ≤ 0.5 %, H2 > H3, no inharmonic above −80 dBc; pink transfer +1.5 ± 0.3 dB at 200 Hz, −1.5 ± 0.3 dB at 10 kHz; toggle loudness change ≤ 0.3 LU. `test_factory_presets` asserts every intent block. Classical & Jazz loses ≤ 0.3 LU LRA on a −10.5 LUFS master with E11 on. Panel perception matches each preset's description.
- **Evidence.** [MacroMap.cpp][MM] `kMusicTable` M5 (`SatDriveDb 9`, Tape); [test_factory_presets.cpp][T-fp] (completeness, keys, ceiling, "does not mute" only); [factory presets][FAC]; roadmap 1.4 in [07][07].

<a id="e21"></a>
#### E21 · Two-stage Smart Volume and Startle Guard (replacing AutoLevel in the night and competitive presets)

*Gaming · benefit 4 · difficulty High · slice 3–5 days, total 4–6 engineer-weeks · Quick win (slice)*

- **Status (Phase 1 batch).** Slice done in `Protection.*` (AutoLevel upper gate, −12…+6 dB, 3 dB/s recovery); Night Mode ambush hole 1–2 s / 5–7.5 s after 5.23 / 2.36 → −0.10 / −0.25 dB, 10 s event 6.89 / 4.01 → 3.25 / −0.40 dB (−0.31 / −0.40 dB since the [E06](#e06) slice: the 3.25 dB depended on the host block). Open (KNOWN_GAP): bed lift 15.77 → 11.98 dB against ≤ +6 dB (Night Mode retune not done), Late Night ±1 LU, the guard.
- **Status (Phase 3 batch 1).** Startle Guard, Dynamic range control and [E20](#e20)'s Tame keying done; Night Mode retuned for the ambience-lift Done-when. `StartleGuard.*` (engine) and `guard.range` (Choice Off / 20 / 15 / 10 LU (Balanced) / 6 LU (Shield), layout version 4, default Off: every other preset renders as before). One control: the Dynamic range is the guard's ceiling over the recent programme. The guard measures the compressor slot's input and turns down its output, so the slot's 0.5 / 1 / 3 ms latency is its look-ahead and no profile's latency changes (checked in all three); it is not a `TruePeakLimiter` sidechain as planned, so that API is unchanged. Sidechain 150 Hz high-pass (the low end is Tame's), −12 dB bell over 2–5 kHz, K-weighting; detector max (BS.1770 momentary, 1 ms mean square − 8 dB); reference a 3 s programme level gated per sample (not during events > 6 LU over it and 300 ms after, silence, or programme > 20 LU under it; 5 s over the gate is a new level), in pre-AutoLevel terms; 150 ms hold, 200 ms release. With the guard on, Competitive FPS on the E60 scene at −24 / −40 LUFS (combat 10.15 / 15.15 LU over the ambience at the input): event over ambience 4.12 / 3.41 LU at 6 LU, 7.95 / 7.41 LU at 10 LU, 12.36 LU at 15 LU (−40) (≤ N + 1 met), onset jump ≤ 0.47 dB, step band 1–2 s after within 0.10 dB, recovery 0 s, bed and step contrast within 0.05 dB of guard off. Unit scene (2 s of fire about 20 LU over −40 dBFS pink): 16.45 / 11.47 / 6.47 / 2.48 LU over the ambience at 20 / 15 / 10 / 6 LU, gain 1 s after −0.02 to −0.36 dB, reference moved 0.01 dB; 3.2 kHz bursts 12 LU over the ambience untouched (0.00 dB) where pink at that loudness goes 5.7 dB down. Night Mode retune: Auto Level target −20 → −14 LUFS with the compressor's 6 dB make-up removed and its threshold (−24 → −18 dB), the upward threshold / floor (−38 / −68 → −32 / −62 dB) and `dyneq.0`'s threshold (−22 → −16 dBFS) moved up with it, guard 20 LU. Ambush KnownGap: bed lift 11.10 → 5.10 dB (closed), hole 1–2 s after −0.07 → −0.05 dB, event +1.18 → −10.31 dB. E60 at −14 / −24 / −40 LUFS: onset jump 0.65 / 3.39 / 0.92 → 0.65 / 1.80 / −0.07 dB; at −24 hole 0.79 → −1.14 dB and recovery 1.38 → 2.43 s (the bed after is louder: asked for +10 dB, Auto Level is still rising to its +6 dB cap at 1 dB/s in the 12 s scene). Track-change KnownGap closed at every level (−14 LUFS +2.00 dB / 1.88 s → −0.16 dB / 0 s, −40 LUFS −3.64 dB / 2.34 s → −1.21 dB / 0 s; −24 still met, −0.95 dB / 0.94 s); silence lift at −24 LUFS 8.60 → 5.83 dB (closed; −14 8.33 → 8.37 open); dialogue SNR −5.86 / −6.18 / −1.31 → −5.75 / −5.44 / −0.32 dB (open). Worse: speech return at −40 LUFS +1.78 → +2.68 dB (open; Auto Level's rise on quiet speech, which the make-up compressor used to take partly back), at −24 0.33 → 0.97 dB (still met); in the E60 matrix the step band after the event at −40 LUFS +2.21 → +2.75 dB (the same rise; −14 / −24 LUFS 0.07 / −0.90 → 0.01 / 0.78 dB). The review re-ran the whole suite against the base build: every other measured value outside Night Mode and E06's Normal-strength case is unchanged. At 10 / 15 LU Night Mode re-opened the track-change gaps (overshoot up to 3.8 / 1.3 dB, settle 2.34 s: the guard catches the loud track from its first snare, its kick being Tame's), hence 20 LU. Render diff: only Night Mode moves (103 values: game-quiet −6.00 dB, ambush −9.53 LU and −12.1 dB true peak, music −2.10 LU, 7.1 −3.20 LU, 31.5–125 Hz bands −2.4 to −4.6 dB with Tame). Open: 10 minutes of mixed programme without leveller oscillation (not measured); Late Night ±1 LU of −20 LUFS and the Podcast & Voice retune; AutoLevel / AutoDrive time constants; sustained programme over the ceiling is modulated at its transients until it is the new level (5 s; a slower release there kept fire 1–4 dB down 1 s after it); no app control beyond the generic grid, and the app's Night hotkey latch (`EngineController::setNight`) still copies the old Night dynamics.
- **Status (Phase 3 batch 2, app).** The app follow-ups are done in `engine/EngineController.*`, `ui/BoostPanel.*` and `ui/ModuleCard.*`. (1) The Night listening hotkey latch (`EngineController::setNight`, [E56](#e56)) copied the old Night dynamics (Auto Level −20 LUFS, compressor −24 dB with +6 dB make-up, upward below −38 dB, no guard); it now reads the Night Mode Gaming factory preset's values (`getNightOverrides`: Auto Level −14 LUFS, `guard.range` 20 LU, compressor −18 dB, knee 10 dB, 3 / 250 ms auto release, no make-up, upward 2.5:1 below −32 dB up to +6 dB, floor −62 dB), so it follows the preset's tuning. Measured with `flubsound-cli process` on the ambush scene (the KnownGap scene rebuilt in Python: Kellet pink at −50 dBFS, 3 s of fire at −12 dBFS peak), batch-1 core, old → new latch on Competitive FPS: bed lift before the event +11.00 → +5.00 dB (Done-when ≤ +6 met), event change +0.73 → −10.88 dB (the fire held by the 20 LU guard), bed 1–2 s after the event −0.08 → −0.05 dB off the bed before (within 1 dB met); Battle Royale +11.07 → +5.07 dB, +1.03 → −10.93 dB, −0.10 → −0.08 dB; on Night Mode Gaming itself the new latch changes nothing (+5.37 / −10.38 / −0.05 dB both ways; the old latch lifted its bed to +11.37 dB). A hotkey only, so no preset render, golden or KnownGap value moves; `tests/app/test_app_night_loopback.cpp` pins the latch to the preset's values, and `test_app_hotkeys.cpp`'s −20 → −14 LUFS check follows it. (2) The Dynamic range control has an app control where a listener finds it: a **Dynamic Range** combo (Off / 20 / 15 / 10 LU (Balanced) / 6 LU (Shield)) with Smoothness in a row under the macros of the Simple view ([06 §3.5](06-gui.md#35-simple-view)), and as a key on the Compressor card of the Advanced view's rack (the guard runs in that slot), not dimmed with the compressor since it acts with it off (`ModuleDescriptor::Key::independent`); `tests/app/test_app_ui_guards.cpp`. Still open from the Phase 3 batch 1 line: the 10-minute oscillation run, Late Night / Podcast & Voice, the AutoLevel / AutoDrive time constants, the guard inside `TruePeakLimiter`, sustained programme over the ceiling, and the −40 LUFS speech-return decision.
- **Status (p3b3, Late Night / Podcast & Voice retune and the 10-minute run).** Partially done: both retunes meet their rows (two presets re-voiced, as the Done-when requires; the owner is told in the release note), the seeded mixed programme exists and its 20 s smoke excerpts meet the oscillation row, and the 10-minute run does not (Auto Level's own time constants, pinned as a KnownGap for the next E21 unit). Tests in the new `tests/test_soak_levels.cpp` (each case ≤ 1.6 s in Release).

  - **Auto Level** (`Protection.*`): (1) the slow measure is 15 s (`AutoLevel::kMeasureMs`, was 3 s), AutoDrive keeps 3 s: 5× apart (a `static_assert`); with both on, a 6 LU step of music is levelled with no swing back (0.61 → 0.00 dB; no factory preset turns both on, Club Loud's AutoDrive is unchanged). (2) The upper gate has a 10 ms detector (14 LU over the measure) beside the 400 ms one (8 LU) and a 300 ms hold, so a burst of fire is held from the block of its first shot; the 400 ms gate alone let the first 200–300 ms in, the measure rose and the rest was admitted: three 1.5 s bursts about 10 LU over a −40 dBFS ambience, lowest gain −3.11 → 6.00 dB, 4 s after 1.95 → 6.00 dB. Nothing is judged before 0.5 s of programme is measured (a first reading from a fade-in held speech out for good). (3) A drop rule (a 3 s measure of the admitted programme 6 LU under the slow one for 3 s restarts it), a 1 dB reversal hysteresis, a 3 s settle after a restart and a 2 s gap rule during it. `GatedLoudness::restart()` is a new audio-thread entry point (`FLUB_NONBLOCKING`, in `tests/test_rtsan.cpp`).
  - **Startle Guard sustained detector** (`StartleGuard.*`): programme over the reference's event gate for 1 s and still within 3 dB of the ceiling is guarded on its momentary loudness alone with a 4 s release; the fast release returns when the loudness falls 3 dB under the ceiling or its target rises 5 dB over the gain. The crest over the last 1 s the plan named does not separate the two (E60 music 11.4 dB, automatic fire 10.7 dB), so the detector keys on the time over the gate instead. KnownGap flipped (new case in `tests/test_dynamics_guard.cpp`): music 15 LU over the ambience at 10 LU, 1.5–4.5 s into it, guard gain swing back **4.88 → 0.80 dB** (gain −5.16 … −0.24 → −4.15 … −1.83 dB). Fire is not kept down: gain 1 s after it −0.02 / −0.10 / −0.24 / −0.36 → −0.04 / −0.15 / −0.29 / −0.41 dB at 20 / 15 / 10 / 6 LU (≤ 1 dB met), over the ambience 16.45 / 11.47 / 6.47 / 2.48 → 17.07 / 12.07 / 7.07 / 3.07 LU (≤ N + 1 met); a full freeze of the release kept the E60 6 LU scene's recovery at 1.03 s (> 1 s), so it was not kept.

Gated: `comp.attack` 3 → 1 ms reads 0.18 / 0.52 / 0.18 dB at −14 / −24 / −40 LUFS; a re-voicing of Night Mode, left to the owner. Smoke (first 20 s) 0.43 / 0.74 / 0.78 / 0 → 0.00 / 0.31 / 0.55 / 0 dB. The guard's gain swing on stationary segments (reported only) 15.43 / 20.63 / 12.39 / 8.07 → 15.19 / 7.30 / 12.02 / 7.48 dB.
